Abstract: Conformal counterfactual inference enables network operators to use logged telemetry to reliably answer 'what-if' questions about network operation. These answers typically take the form of prediction sets that contain, with a user-defined probability, the key performance indicators (KPIs) that would have been observed under alternative control actions. A key challenge is that logged telemetry may omit variables used by the controller, resulting in hidden confounding and invalidating the statistical guarantees of counterfactual analysis. In principle, this issue can be addressed using randomized telemetry, collected by assigning control actions independently of the network state. However, because such randomization may disrupt normal operation, randomized telemetry is typically scarce, causing counterfactual analysis based solely on it to produce uninformative prediction sets. To address these challenges, we propose Confounding-Valid Counterfactual Conformal Inference (CV-CCI), which combines abundant, potentially confounded observational telemetry with limited randomized data through the General Synthetic-Powered Inference (GESPI) principle. CV-CCI leverages observational data to improve efficiency while using randomized data to retain finite-sample coverage guarantees under arbitrary hidden confounding. Experiments on two representative radio access network (RAN) control tasks show that CV-CCI remains valid under hidden confounding while producing more efficient prediction sets than state-of-the-art confounding-valid baselines.

</details>


### [62] [Deep Microcompression: Structured Pruning and Bit-packed Quantization for Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05081)
*Opegbemi Matthias Busoye,Tolulope Matthew Busoye,Eghonghon-aye Eigbe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DMC（Deep Microcompression），一种面向裸机微控制器的硬件感知深度学习推理流水线，集成结构化剪枝、量化感知训练和定长比特打包，在LeNet-5上实现55.8倍权重压缩比（98.77%准确率），并在RP2040上相比TensorFlow Lite将二进制大小缩减3倍，同时首次实现在仅2KB SRAM的ATmega328P上部署标准CNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源极度受限的裸机微控制器（如仅2KB SRAM的ATmega328P）上运行CNN推理此前被认为不可行，现有框架如TensorFlow Lite在二进制尺寸和内存占用上仍无法满足这类超低资源设备的约束，亟需一种硬件感知的压缩与部署流水线来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: DMC采用硬件感知的三阶段流水线：首先进行结构化剪枝以减少冗余连接，其次结合量化感知训练将权重映射到低比特精度，最后通过定长比特打包实现高效的存储布局。整个流程生成无依赖的C语言库，确保推理延迟具有确定性。

Result: 在LeNet-5上实现55.8倍权重压缩比并保持98.77%准确率；在RP2040（Cortex-M0+）上相比TensorFlow Lite将二进制大小缩减3倍且准确率持平；首次在仅2KB SRAM的ATmega328P上成功部署标准CNN，此前被认为在该设备上不可行。

Conclusion: DMC证明了通过硬件感知的剪枝、量化和比特打包协同设计，标准CNN推理可以下沉到极低资源的裸机微控制器上，为边缘AI在超低功耗、超小内存设备上的部署开辟了新路径。

Abstract: This paper introduces Deep Microcompression (DMC), a hardware-aware pipeline for deep learning inference on bare-metal microcontrollers. DMC integrates structured pruning, quantization-aware training, and fixed-length bit-packing to achieve a 55.8$\times$ weight compression ratio on LeNet-5 (98.77\% accuracy), generating a dependency-free C library with deterministic latency. On the RP2040 (Cortex-M0+), DMC reduces binary size by 3$\times$ versus TensorFlow Lite while matching its accuracy. Critically, DMC enables the first documented deployment of a standard CNN on the ATmega328P, a device constrained to 2KB SRAM, previously considered infeasible for CNN inference.

</details>


### [63] [NEAT-POCKET: Pocket-Conditioned Autoregressive 3D Molecular Generation with a Neighborhood-Guided Set Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05097)
*Roxane Axel Jacob,Daniel Rose,Thierry Langer,Johannes Kirchmair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NEAT-POCKET是基于自回归NEAT模型的扩展框架，用于3D分子生成，能在蛋白质口袋环境中逐原子生成分子，并支持片段补全任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的全新分子设计有望加速早期药物发现，但现有方法在生成速度、原子置换不变性和氢原子建模等方面存在局限。

Method: 在自回归NEAT模型基础上引入蛋白质口袋条件约束，实现逐原子3D分子生成，同时保持原子置换不变性并显式建模氢原子。

Result: 在CrossDocked和SPINDR数据集上的基准测试中，NEAT-POCKET获得了具有竞争力的基于结构的生成性能，且采样速度显著快于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: NEAT-POCKET是一个快速、灵活且实用的基于结构的药物设计框架，不仅支持全分子生成，还能自然应用于与先导化合物优化和骨架扩展直接相关的口袋条件片段补全。

Abstract: AI-driven de novo molecular design offers a promising route to accelerate early-stage drug discovery by generating novel ligands directly within target protein binding pockets. We present NEAT-POCKET, a pocket-conditioned extension of the autoregressive NEAT model for 3D molecular generation. NEAT-POCKET generates molecules atom by atom in protein pocket environments while preserving atom permutation invariance and explicitly modeling hydrogen atoms. Benchmarks on the CrossDocked and SPINDR datasets show that NEAT-POCKET achieves competitive structure-based generation performance while sampling substantially faster than existing baselines. Beyond full-molecule generation, NEAT-POCKET naturally enables pocket-conditioned fragment completion, a task directly relevant to lead optimization and scaffold elaboration. These results position NEAT-POCKET as a fast, flexible, and practical framework for structure-based drug design.

</details>


### [64] [A Comparative Study of Counterfactual Explainers for Graph Neural Networks Enabling Multiple Types of Graph Edit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05113)
*Maria Myrto Villia,Filippos Gouidis,Theodore Patkos,Panos Trahanias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了六种最先进的图结构数据反事实解释方法，在真实和合成数据集上覆盖二分类和多分类的图/节点分类任务，使用多种定量和定性指标评估其性能，旨在揭示各方法的优劣以指导未来研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构数据上的反事实解释旨在找到使模型预测改变的最小和现实的图修改，但目前缺乏通用且高效的方法，现有方法在解释大小、覆盖率和质量之间各有取舍，因此需要系统的比较研究来识别各方法的优势和不足。

Method: 对六种SOTA反事实解释模型进行全面的实证比较，在多样化的真实世界和合成数据集上开展实验，覆盖二分类和多分类的图分类与节点分类任务，并采用多种定量和定性指标进行评估。

Result: 研究揭示了各方法在不同任务和数据条件下的性能差异，识别出每种方法擅长的场景及其不足，表明现有方法在解释大小、覆盖率和质量之间存在权衡关系。

Conclusion: 图反事实解释领域仍需进一步研究，特别是在开发更通用和高效的算法方面；现有方法的优势互补性为未来工作提供了明确的方向。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations for graph-structured data seek to determine minimal and realistic modifications required in an input graph to alter a model's prediction to a predefined output. Although counterfactual explainers that support modifying the graph by both adding and removing edges have recently emerged, there is still a lack of general and efficient methods, especially when considering the quality of the generated explanations. Moreover, the problem remains far from solved, as existing methods exhibit different strengths and weaknesses, often trading off between explanation size, coverage and quality. For this reason, it is important to identify where each method performs well and where it falls short, so as to guide future research in the field. Thus, our study compares six state-of-the-art (SOTA) models on a diverse set of real-world and synthetic datasets, covering both binary and multi-class graph and node classification tasks, and evaluates their performance using diverse quantitative and qualitative metrics.

</details>


### [65] [Single-Query Black-Box Calibration Auditing via Logit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05125)
*Roman Plaud,Antoine Saillenfest,Matthieu Labeau,Thomas Bonald,Willem Waegeman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用LLM API的logit_bias参数来绕过连续概率输出限制的方法，仅用每样本一次查询即可估计精确概率阈值，并提出了二元任务下真实校准误差（TCE）的一致估计量，实现了对黑盒基础模型的高效审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业API提供商越来越多地隐藏连续输出概率，而标准校准度量需要这些概率。为了准确评估LLM作为零样本分类器的校准性能，需要一种在不获取原始概率的情况下进行校准审计的方法。

Method: 利用任何暴露logit_bias参数的LLM API，通过数学操纵该参数来精确评估概率阈值，每次样本仅需一次查询。基于此机制，提出了二元任务下真实校准误差（TCE）的新颖且可证明一致的估计器。

Result: 提出的估计器是统计一致的，能够仅通过最少的查询次数（每样本一次）在隐藏概率的API环境中准确评估校准误差，为黑盒模型审计提供了可行途径。

Conclusion: 该方法有效克服了商业API隐藏输出概率带来的透明度问题，为黑盒基础模型的校准审计提供了一套高效、可证明有效的框架。

Abstract: Evaluating the calibration of Large Language Models (LLMs) is critical for their safe deployment as zero-shot classifiers. Yet, commercial API providers increasingly hide the continuous output probabilities required by standard calibration metrics. To bypass this opacity, we demonstrate that any LLM API exposing a logit\_bias parameter can be mathematically manipulated to evaluate exact probability thresholds using strictly one query per sample. Leveraging this mechanism, we introduce a novel and provably consistent estimator of the True Calibration Error for binary tasks. Our approach therefore provides an efficient framework for auditing black-box foundation models.

</details>


### [66] [Coarse-Graining Hidden Representations: Unsupervised Neuron Selection via Mapping Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05126)
*Margherita Mele,Andrea Castagna,Roberto Menichetti,Raffaello Potestio,Alessandro Ingrosso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用映射熵（ME）在无监督条件下对过参数化神经网络进行神经元选择（粗粒化），通过最小化ME保留信息量最大的神经元子集。实验表明，选出的子网络在教师-学生网络、非线性高斯过程和MNIST任务上均优于随机等规模子集，尤其在强压缩下优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化神经网络包含远超任务需要的隐藏单元，但哪些神经元是关键的、这种区分能否在无标签和无梯度的表征层面被识别，仍是一个未解问题。

Method: 将神经元选择建模为对隐藏层进行粗粒化的问题，即保留一部分神经元。用映射熵（ME）量化丢弃部分神经元所损失的判别能力，选择最小化ME的神经元子集作为最有信息的表示。该方法完全无监督，仅依赖隐藏激活的统计信息。

Result: 在教师-学生网络中，ME优化能恢复最小教师一致表示，并依据残留变异性保留额外神经元；在非线性高斯过程任务中能选出功能类映射一致的神经元子集；在翻译增强的MNIST上，ME选择的子网络在等规模随机子集的比较中表现更优，尤其在高压缩率下效果显著。

Conclusion: ME提供了一种无需标签或梯度的神经元重要性度量，将构型可区分性与预测性能联系起来，为理解过参数化网络的冗余结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: Overparameterized neural networks carry far more hidden units than a task nominally requires, raising the question of which neurons are essential and whether that distinction is legible in the representation itself, without labels or gradients. We cast neuron selection as the problem of coarse-graining the hidden layer by retaining a subset of its neurons, and score each putative selection by the mapping entropy (ME). This quantity measures the loss of discriminatory power inherent in discarding part of the network neurons, and the selection that minimises the ME is taken as particularly informative. This criterion is fully unsupervised, in that it depends only on hidden-activation statistics. In teacher-student networks, ME optimisation recovers the minimal teacher-consistent representation and retains extra units in proportion to the hidden layer's residual variability; in a non-linear Gaussian process task, it selects coherent functional-class mappings whose preferred class shifts across training. On this task and on translation-augmented MNIST, ME-selected subnetworks outperform random subsets of equal size, most clearly under strong compression - linking configurational distinguishability to predictive performance.

</details>


### [67] [MomentQuant: an even more minimalist interval method with linear time complexity for time series classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05136)
*Johann Faouzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MomentQuant算法，通过优化Quant算法的实现和使用Cornish-Fisher展开近似分位数替代精确分位数，显著提升了时间序列分类算法的运行速度，仅以极小的预测性能损失为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列分类在众多实际应用中需求日益增长，现有算法在预测性能和计算成本之间存在权衡。Quant算法虽然精度高且速度快，但仍有进一步提升速度的空间，尤其是在实际应用中推理（inference）次数远多于训练（training）次数的场景下，更快的推理速度具有重要意义。

Method: 提出两项改进：(1) 对原始Quant算法进行更好的优化实现，保持算法逻辑不变但提升执行效率；(2) 提出MomentQuant算法，使用Cornish-Fisher展开推导近似分位数来代替精确分位数，从而消除对时间序列排序的需求，降低计算复杂度。

Result: 实验证据表明：优化的Quant实现比原始Quant更快速；MomentQuant比优化的Quant实现更快，但预测性能仅有极小的下降。这些改进在推理频繁的实际应用场景中尤为有价值。

Conclusion: 通过算法实现优化和近似分位数推导两种策略，可以在保持高度分类精度的同时显著提升时间序列分类的推理速度，适用于对速度敏感的实际应用场景。

Abstract: Time series data is very common in many real-world applications and in numerous domains, with increasing interest for automated information extraction using machine learning. One of these subfields is time series classification, which consists in assigning a label to each new, unseen time series. Many algorithms have been developed over the past decades, with the trade-off between predictive performance and computational cost being consistently discussed. Quant, an interval-based algorithm extracting quantiles from recursive, fixed, dyadic intervals, was shown to achieve high accuracy, while being very fast. We propose two changes to make this algorithm even faster. The first one is a better optimized implementation of the exact same algorithm. The second one is to derive approximate quantiles, using the Cornish-Fisher expansion, instead of exact quantiles. This change removes the necessity to sort the time series, leading to a smaller computational complexity. We call this novel algorithm MomentQuant. We provide evidence that our implementation of Quant is faster than the original one, and that MomentQuant is even faster than our implementation of Quant, at the cost of a tiny decrease in predictive performance. These improvements are especially relevant for real-life applications, where inference is performed much more often than training.

</details>


### [68] [From 80x to 385x: A Best-Matching-Unit Search at the L2 Roof, Measured Against a Symmetrically Tuned Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05138)
*Andrew James Amos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对GPU实现的比较不对称问题（一方经过调优而另一方未调优），对新型SOM算法SparseBin及基线算法cuSPARSE均进行了系统性调优。通过四个调优杠杆（tile size、tile成员聚类、神经元轴分块和向量化加载），SparseBin每epoch速度提升5.6-10.1倍，对CUDA实现的优势从~80倍扩大到~385倍。同时，对cuSPARSE施以所有可类比杠杆后也提速2-3倍。最终调优后的kernel达到L2带宽峰值77%，所有其他单元处于40-65%，剩余提升空间被限制在~1.3倍，属于终局性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU实现之间的性能比较常常存在不公平性：通常只有一方（作者自己的算法）经过精心调优，而基线算法只是按原样运行，导致比较结果失真。

Method: 作者提出SparseBin（新型SOM算法），并通过四个调优杠杆进行优化：tile size、tile-membership clustering、neuron-axis chunking和vectorised loads。同时，对基线算法cuSPARSE施加所有具有对应物的调优杠杆，实现双向公平调优。

Result: SparseBin在32x32到512x512的map尺寸下每epoch提速5.6-10.1倍，相对CUDA实现的优势从~80倍提升至~385倍。cuSPARSE经调优后提速2-3倍。调优后的kernel达到L2带宽峰值77%，其余单元在40-65%，理论上限为~1.3倍提升空间。

Conclusion: 通过对双方进行对称调优，SparseBin的优势依然显著，且高性能基线验证了结果的可靠性。调优结果达到L2带宽瓶颈，说明已接近硬件的理论极限，属于终局性结果而非中间节点。

Abstract: Comparisons between GPU implementations are usually asymmetric: one side is tuned by its author, the other is run as found. I report a programme that tuned both a novel SOM algorithm (SparseBin) and the baseline algorithm it was being compared to (cuSPARSE). The best-matching-unit search that dominates self-organizing map training was tuned through four levers - tile size, tile-membership clustering, neuron-axis chunking and vectorised loads - reaching 5.6-10.1x per epoch over the previously published configuration at map sizes from 32x32 to 512x512, and lifting the margin over the CUDA implementation behind our earlier MEDLINE atlases from ~80x to ~385x. cuSPARSE, the implementation SparseBin is compared against, received every lever with an analogue on its side, and became 2-3x faster in the process. The tuned kernel pressed the L2 bandwidth roof at 77% of peak with every other unit at 40-65%, bounding any further lever at ~1.3x - a terminal result rather than a waypoint, and every untested lever was either capped by that bound by construction or measured null.

</details>


### [69] [Beyond Stationarity in Time Series: Discovering Causal Structures and Latent Regimes via Markov Blankets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05150)
*Lei Zan,Charles K. Assaad,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RCBNB-MB算法，一种面向时间序列的因果发现方法，它放松了单一、时间一致因果结构的假设，通过迭代分段识别潜在的因果状态（regimes），并在每个状态内利用马尔可夫毯发现因果图，从而处理非平稳时间序列中的因果结构变化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列通常在离散时间点观测，且现实动态系统中常出现状态转换（regime changes），导致传统的静态因果结构假设不再成立，而现有方法大多假设单一且时间一致的因果结构，难以处理非平稳动态系统中的因果发现问题。

Method: RCBNB-MB采用迭代策略：先将时间序列分割为不同的因果状态（subset of time points with stable causal structure），再在每个状态内发现因果图。算法利用马尔可夫毯（Markov blanket）而非直接父节点进行因果发现，以增强对因果发现错误的鲁棒性并保留预测信息。作者同时提供了算法在恢复状态转换和因果图方面的理论保证。

Result: 在已知真实结构的模拟数据集和真实IT监控数据上的大量实验表明，RCBNB-MB在准确检测状态变化及其关联因果图方面系统性优于基线方法，验证了其在非平稳时间序列分析中的鲁棒性和通用性。

Conclusion: RCBNB-MB为处理非平稳时间序列中的时变因果结构提供了一个鲁棒且灵活的框架，通过状态识别与马尔可夫毯结合的策略，在理论和实证层面均展现了有效性，特别适用于需要考虑状态转换的实际场景（如IT监控）。

Abstract: This paper introduces Regime-aware Constraint-Based and Noise-Based causal discovery with Markov Blankets (RCBNB-MB), a novel causal discovery algorithm for time series that relaxes the common assumption of a single, time-consistent causal structure. Time series are typically observed at discrete time points and often exhibit regime changes that challenge the assumption of a static causal structure, a limitation in many real-world dynamic systems. To address this challenge, RCBNB-MB identifies latent causal regimes, defined as subsets of time points within which a stable causal structure holds. The algorithm follows an iterative strategy that segments the time series into regimes and discovers the causal graph within each regime. By leveraging the Markov blanket rather than direct parents, RCBNB-MB gains robustness to errors in causal discovery and preserves predictive information. We provide theoretical guarantees for RCBNB-MB's ability to recover both regime transitions and causal graphs under reasonable assumptions. Furthermore, we validate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on simulated datasets with known ground truth and real-world IT monitoring data, where taking into account regime shifts is critical. Empirical results show that RCBNB-MB systematically outperforms baseline approaches in accurately detecting regime changes and their associated causal graphs, positioning it as a robust and versatile framework for non-stationary time series analysis.

</details>


### [70] [Phase Transition Frequency as a Training Time Predictor of Test Accuracy in ResNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05194)
*Arunan J*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证检验了ResNet微调过程中离散类别可分性跳跃次数作为最终测试准确率预测指标的可靠性。在75个实验（覆盖4个基准和3种架构）中，发现跳跃次数在标准i.i.d.基准上与最终准确率呈现强负相关（CIFAR-10上r=-0.84，CIFAR-100上r=-0.87），但在分布偏移条件下相关性显著减弱。部分相关分析表明在控制架构深度后跳跃次数仍具预测力，但与六种替代训练曲线信号比较后发现，其在压力基准下被其他信号超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望找到一种廉价、易于在标准训练循环中记录的指标，用于在训练过程中早期预测模型的最终测试准确率，特别是通过观察类别可分性跳跃次数来探索其作为训练质量探针的可行性。

Method: 在75个实验中，跨越四个基准（CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet、CIFAR-10-C）和三种ResNet架构（18/50/101），每个配置使用5-10个种子进行实验。计算离散类别可分性跳跃次数与最终测试准确率的相关性；通过部分相关分析控制架构深度；并与六种替代训练曲线信号进行对比。

Result: 标准i.i.d.基准上跳跃次数与最终准确率呈强负相关（CIFAR-10: r=-0.84, CIFAR-100: r=-0.87），但TinyImageNet上降至r=-0.45，CIFAR-10-C上降至r=-0.19。部分相关分析在CIFAR-100上保留显著预测力（r_partial=-0.69, p=0.007）。在CIFAR-100和CIFAR-10上跳跃次数是最强或最强之一的信号，但在压力基准下被其他信号超越。

Conclusion: 类别可分性跳跃次数可作为分布内训练质量的廉价探针，尤其适用于标准i.i.d.分类任务；但在分布偏移条件下预测力不足。该发现仅限于训练曲线级信号，与当前文献中更强的竞争者（如有效秩、Hessian锐度、Fisher信息等）的比较尚待研究。

Abstract: The number of discrete class-separability jumps observed during ResNet finetuning is examined empirically as a predictor of final test accuracy. Across 75 experiments spanning four benchmarks (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and CIFAR-10-C) and three architectures (ResNet-18, ResNet-50, and ResNet-101), with five to ten seeds per configuration, a strong within-dataset negative correlation is obtained on standard i.i.d. classification benchmarks: \(r = -0.84\) on CIFAR-10 (\(p < 10^{-8}\), \(n = 30\)) and \(r = -0.87\) on CIFAR-100 (\(p < 10^{-5}\), \(n = 15\)). Under distributional stress, the relationship attenuates: TinyImageNet yields \(r = -0.45\), and the CIFAR-10-C corruption benchmark yields \(r = -0.19\). Two additional analyses discipline the empirical claim. A partial correlation controlling for architecture depth, treated as a linear covariate, shows that on CIFAR-100 the transition count retains statistically significant predictive power (\(r_{\mathrm{partial}} = -0.69\), \(p = 0.007\)); the corresponding result under the stricter categorical conditioning is not established at \(n = 15\). A comparison against six alternative training-curve signals shows that transition count achieved the strongest correlation among the evaluated signals on CIFAR-100 and one of the strongest on CIFAR-10, but is dominated by other signals on the two stressed benchmarks. The comparison is restricted to training-curve-level signals; comparisons against effective rank, Hessian sharpness, Fisher information, margin, and neural-collapse measures, which are the strongest competitors in the current literature, are not part of the present study and remain open. The observation is presented as an in-distribution training-quality probe among a family of candidate probes, and an inexpensive detection procedure suitable for logging alongside a standard training loop is provided.

</details>


### [71] [Dimension-Adaptive Batched Lipschitz Narrowing Without Knowing the Zooming Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05214)
*Yasong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Count-Adaptive BLiN算法，消除了原A-BLiN算法中ACE序列对缩放维度$d_z$的依赖，通过计数淘汰后存活的立方体数量来选择下一条边长，在不需要知道$d_z$和缩放常数$C_z$的情况下，仍能达到$\widetilde{\mathcal O}_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})$的遗憾界和$\mathcal O_d(\log\log T)$的批次复杂度，证明了最优批次复杂度仍为$\Theta_d(\log\log T)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原A-BLiN算法中的ACE序列依赖于缩放维度$d_z$这一先验知识，但实际应用中$d_z$往往是未知的，这限制了算法的适用性。作者希望设计一种不依赖$d_z$的算法，同时保持原有的理论保证。

Method: 提出Count-Adaptive BLiN算法，核心思想是用上一轮淘汰后幸存的立方体数量来决定下一条边的长度，从而完全摆脱对$d_z$和缩放常数$C_z$的依赖。这是一种自适应的边长选择机制，利用计数信息而非显式的维度参数来驱动算法。

Result: Count-Adaptive BLiN在$d_z$未知的条件下，实现了$\widetilde{\mathcal O}_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})$的遗憾界（与原A-BLiN量级一致）和$\mathcal O_d(\log\log T)$的批次复杂度。结合原文Theorem 10的自适应网格下界，证明当$d_z$未知时最优批次复杂度仍为$\Theta_d(\log\log T)$。

Conclusion: 该结果说明$d_z$的先验知识对A-BLiN并非必要，通过巧妙的计数自适应策略即可消除这一依赖，同时保持最优的理论保证，使算法在实际中更具鲁棒性和适用性。

Abstract: The Appropriately Combined Edge-length (ACE) sequence in A-BLiN depends on the zooming dimension $d_z$. This note removes that dependence. The next edge length is selected from the number of cubes that survive the preceding elimination. The resulting Count-Adaptive BLiN algorithm does not use $d_z$ or the zooming constant $C_z$, yet it attains $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})$ regret with $\mathcal O_d(\log\log T)$ batches. Together with the adaptive-grid lower bound in Theorem 10 of the original paper, the optimal batch complexity remains $Θ_d(\log\log T)$ when $d_z$ is unknown.

</details>


### [72] [FedDRAW: Federated Dual Reputation Annealing Weighting for Heterogeneous Multi-Institutional Chest Radiograph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05223)
*Maryam Moradpour,Anne-Christin Hauschild*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedDRAW（联邦双声誉退火加权）方法，一种服务器端聚合策略，结合数据量先验与客户端-全局参数的余弦相似度，通过双退火调度动态调整客户端加权，在胸部X光片数据集上显著优于七种联邦基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中FedAvg按样本量加权聚合，导致数据量少但信息量高的医院影响力被固化地压制，而大数据量的客户端即使信息量低也会主导全局模型，造成模型偏置。

Method: FedDRAW在服务器端结合数据量先验与客户端-全局参数的余弦相似度，采用两层耦合退火调度——内层调度将客户端声誉从数据量先验向相似度过渡，外层延迟退火调度控制softmax逆温度，使早期和中期的加权保持选择性，最终收敛时趋于均匀。

Result: 在12种模拟客户端分区场景、两个胸部X光数据集（CheXpert和ChestMNIST）上，与七个联邦基线方法对比。FedDRAW在AUC和特异度-灵敏度几何均值(GM)两项指标上均取得最高平均排名，Friedman检验与Nemenyi事后分析确认差异具有统计学显著性。

Conclusion: 通过调度两种信号而非仅依赖样本量固定权重，FedDRAW能够降低诊断模型的偏置，为联邦医学诊断提供更公平、信息量感知的聚合方案。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence models are promising for medical diagnosis, but they require large numbers of unbiased data, which in medicine are distributed across hospitals and cannot be centralized to protect patient privacy. Federated Learning (FL) addresses this, since hospitals train one shared diagnostic model while patient data remain local. Training proceeds in communication rounds, in which each hospital trains the shared model locally and returns it to the server for merging by weighted average. This aggregation weight determines whose institutional knowledge shapes the result. Federated averaging (FedAvg) sets it in proportion to local sample count, so a small but informative hospital is permanently assigned a small influence, andl argest clients could dominate the global model even when they are less informative. We propose Federated Dual Reputation Annealing Weighting (FedDRAW), a server-side aggregation method that combines a data-size prior with the cosine similarity between client and global parameters under two coupled annealing schedules. An inner schedule shifts client reputation from the size prior towards similarity. An outer, deferred annealing schedule on the softmax inverse temperature keeps the weighting selective in the early and middle rounds and relaxes it to uniformity at convergence. We evaluate FedDRAW on 12 simulated client-partition scenarios of two chest radiograph datasets (CheXpert and ChestMNIST), against seven federated baselines under identical local training settings. FedDRAW achieved the highest average rank among all eight methods under both AUC and the geometric mean (GM) of sensitivity and specificity, which a Friedman test with Nemenyi post-hoc analysis confirmed to be a statistically significant difference between the methods. Scheduling two signals, rather than fixing the weights by sample count alone, could enable less biased diagnostic models.

</details>


### [73] [Hessian-based molecular conformation augmentation for a scalable and efficient strategy of machine learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05233)
*Bumju Kwak,Jeonghee Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于Hessian导出的数据增强方案（UniAug和ModeAug），通过泰勒展开实现无需修改训练目标和反向传播图的有效增强，可即插即用地集成到现有MLIP架构中，提升模型精度并针对不同任务提供实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习原子间势（MLIP）通常仅基于能量和原子力训练，忽略了对振动分析和过渡态搜索等应用至关重要的Hessian信息。现有将Hessian纳入训练目标的方法需要修改架构并因高阶反向传播带来巨大计算和内存开销。

Method: 提出两种基于Hessian导出的数据增强方案：(1) 各向同性高斯位移增强（UniAug）；(2) 简正模式加权位移增强（ModeAug）。两种方法均利用简单泰勒展开实现，不改动训练目标，也不扩展自微分图，因而可以无缝、即插即用地集成到现有架构和训练流程中。

Result: 在非平衡和平衡数据集上的综合评估表明，所提方法能够提升模型精度，同时为实际应用提供任务特定的指导准则。

Conclusion: 该方法有效克服了现有利用Hessian信息的瓶颈，以轻量级数据增强方式将曲率信息注入MLIP训练，在不改变训练框架的前提下提升模型性能，并具备良好的通用性与实用性。

Abstract: While machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have successfully learned potential energy surfaces (PES) and atomic forces, many practical applications, such as vibrational analysis and transition state search, rely heavily on the PES Hessian. Yet, standard MLIPs tend to be trained on energy and forces alone, leaving Hessian information largely unexploited. Meanwhile, existing methods that explicitly incorporate the Hessian into training objectives require architectural modifications and introduce significant computational and memory overheads due to higher-order backpropagation. To address these limitations, we propose two Hessian-derived data augmentation schemes: isotropic Gaussian displacement (\textbf{UniAug}) and normal mode-weighted displacement (\textbf{ModeAug}). Both methods utilize simple Taylor expansions, achieving effective augmentation without altering training objectives or extending the autograd graph. This allows seamless, plug-and-play integration with existing architectures and training pipelines. Comprehensive evaluations across non-equilibrium and equilibrium datasets demonstrate that our approach enhances model accuracy while providing practical, task-specific guidelines.

</details>


### [74] [PRICE: A Systematic Study of LLM Adaptation Choices for Bitcoin Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05235)
*Maryam Fakhari,Mehran Safayani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRICE框架，通过系统化的LLM适配策略（包括LoRA微调、递归多步推理、整数化数值表示、CTF提示和零温度解码）实现短期比特币价格预测，在八个基线模型的对比中取得最低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币市场具有极端波动性和非平稳特性，传统预测方法难以应对。尽管LLM在时间序列预测中展现了潜力，但金融场景下多种适配策略的组合效应尚未被充分探索，缺乏对微调、数值表示、提示工程、推理和解码等环节系统性影响的研究。

Method: PRICE基于4比特量化LLaMA-3 8B模型，集成了五个核心组件：LoRA参数高效微调、递归多步推理、整数化数值表示、CTF提示策略以及零温度精确解码。通过消融实验逐一验证每个组件的贡献，并与八种transformer和时序基础模型进行比较评估。

Result: 消融实验表明每个组件均对预测准确性和可靠性有贡献。LoRA实现有限硬件上的高效训练，递归推理提升精度，整数化降低误差，CTF提示优于Chain-of-Thought、隐式Chain-of-Thought和few-shot提示，零温度解码增强递归预测稳定性。PRICE在验证集和测试集均取得最低预测误差。

Conclusion: 尽管基础模型主要基于文本而非时序数据预训练，PRICE仍能达到或超越专门的时序基础模型。研究表明适配策略的选择对LLM在数值时间序列预测中的准确性和鲁棒性起着决定性作用。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency markets exhibit extreme volatility and non-stationary dynamics that challenge conventional forecasting methods. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for time series forecasting, the combined effects of adaptation choices remain largely unexplored in financial settings. This study introduces PRICE, a structured approach for adapting LLMs to short-term Bitcoin price forecasting. Built on a 4-bit quantized LLaMA-3 8B model, PRICE investigates how fine-tuning, numerical representation, prompting, inference, and decoding jointly influence forecasting performance. PRICE integrates Parameter-efficient fine-tuning with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), Recursive multi-step inference, Integer-rounded numerical representation, Context-Task-Format (CTF) prompting, and Exact zero-temperature decoding. Ablation studies show that each component contributes to forecasting accuracy and reliability. LoRA enables efficient training on limited hardware, recursive inference improves accuracy, integer-rounded values reduce errors, CTF prompting outperforms Chain-of-Thought, Implicit Chain-of-Thought (iCoT), and few-shot prompting, and zero-temperature decoding improves stability during recursive forecasting. Comparative evaluation against eight transformer-based and time-series foundation models shows that PRICE achieves the lowest forecasting errors on both validation and test sets while maintaining robust performance across evaluation periods. Despite being based on a model primarily pretrained on text rather than time-series data, PRICE achieves competitive or superior performance relative to specialized foundation models. These findings demonstrate that adaptation choices critically determine the accuracy and robustness of LLMs for numerical time-series forecasting.

</details>


### [75] [GLASS: Graph-Language Alignment with Spherical Scoring for Transferable Graph-Level Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05253)
*Xudong Wang,Chris Ding,Tongxin Li,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GLASS是一个面向图级异常检测的框架，通过图-语言在单位超球面上的对齐实现跨域迁移，利用Graph Descriptor Prompt序列化图属性，结合多切片软余弦目标和Spherical Multi-Modal Scoring实现零样本及少样本异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图级异常检测方法通常局限于单一领域或需要大量目标域标注数据，跨域泛化能力不足，难以在实际应用中应对多样化的图结构数据。

Method: GLASS包含三个核心组件：(1) 通过多切片软余弦目标在单位超球面上对齐结构感知图编码器与指令感知文本嵌入，构建统一表征空间；(2) 将局部、全局和语义属性序列化为Graph Descriptor Prompt作为文本桥梁，并通过Matryoshka表征切片保证多尺度一致性；(3) 提出Spherical Multi-Modal Scoring，使用von Mises-Fisher核密度估计在图像文本嵌入空间中进行异常评分，其高浓度极限可退化为角度k近邻评分。

Result: 在十二个基准数据集和三个元域上的实验表明，GLASS取得了最佳平均AUROC和排名，超越了现有先进的GLAD基线方法，并展现了有效的跨域迁移能力。

Conclusion: GLASS通过图-语言对齐与概率密度估计的创新结合，为图级异常检测提供了一种具有强跨域可迁移性的统一框架，零样本和少样本场景下均表现出色，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: We introduce GLASS, a framework for graph-level anomaly detection (GLAD) that achieves robust cross-domain transferability through graph-language alignment on the unit hypersphere. GLASS builds a unified representation space by aligning a structure-aware graph encoder with an instruction-aware text embedding via a multi-slice soft cosine objective. Our framework serializes local, global, and semantic graph properties into a compact Graph Descriptor Prompt (GraphDP), creating a text bridge that enables domain-agnostic anomaly scoring. By enforcing multi-scale consistency through Matryoshka representation slices, the model captures anomalous deviations at multiple levels of granularity. For scoring, we formulate anomaly detection as density estimation on the aligned hypersphere and introduce Spherical Multi-Modal Scoring (SMS), which instantiates von Mises-Fisher kernel density estimators in both graph and text embedding spaces. This probabilistic formulation recovers angular k-nearest-neighbor scoring as a high-concentration limiting case and provides a principled fusion of structural and semantic anomaly signals. The shared text embedding space further serves as a cross-domain bridge: by encoding a target domain's GraphDP without target-domain training data, GLASS performs zero-shot anomaly detection, and with only a handful of normal examples, few-shot adaptation via reference-set calibration. Across twelve benchmarks and three meta-domains, GLASS obtains the best average AUROC and rank compared with recent advanced GLAD baselines and enables effective cross-domain transfer.

</details>


### [76] [How to Speculate about Uncertainty in Agentic Coding? A Draft-Model Gate Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05274)
*Konstantin Grotov,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Speculative Uncertainty (SU)方法，通过对黑箱LLM智能体的输出token进行单次前向评分，在不访问logits、权重、激活值或重复采样的条件下，恢复预测性失败信号，从而在代价高昂的执行前识别智能体的错误行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在软件工程任务中部署时经常"自信地犯错"，不良行为只有在昂贵的执行和重试之后才会被发现，造成大量资源浪费。现有的不确定性估计方法通常需要访问模型内部信息或多次采样，不适用于黑箱部署场景。

Method: 逆向投机解码的思想：利用小型开源draft模型对智能体已生成的轨迹进行单次前向评分，获得投机性交叉似然（speculative cross-likelihoods）。通过分离推理和行动片段提取相感知特征（phase-aware features），并针对可验证目标进行校准，生成失败似然分数。随后将该分数用于预执行否决门控（pre-execution veto gate）策略。

Result: 在Qwen3-Coder-480B和Claude 3.5 Sonnet两个软件工程智能体上，执行错误率降低6-8个百分点，token成本降低14-19%，方法可迁移到分布外基准测试且无需重新训练，并能跨智能体模型泛化。

Conclusion: SU方法证明从黑箱智能体的输出token中提取可操作的失败预测信号是可行且高效的，为下游策略（如路由、人工干预或额外测试时计算）提供了可直接消费的失败风险分数。

Abstract: LLM agents deployed for software engineering fail expensively: they act confidently wrong, and bad actions are recognized only after costly execution and retry. We present Speculative Uncertainty (SU), a method that recovers a predictive failure signal for a black-box agent from its output tokens alone, with no access to logits, weights, activations, or repeated sampling. Inverting speculative decoding, a small open-weight draft model scores the agent's already-generated trajectory in a single forward pass. From these speculative cross-likelihoods we extract phase-aware features by separating the reasoning and action spans, and calibrate them against a verifiable objective. SU produces a failure-likelihood score that any downstream policy, such as routing, human intervention, or extra test-time compute, can consume directly. To show the signal is actionable, we instantiate one such policy, a pre-execution veto gate, on software engineering agents Qwen3-Coder-480B and closed-source Claude 3.5 Sonnet, cutting execution error rate by 6-8 percentage points and token cost by 14-19% in deployment, transferring to out-of-distribution benchmarks without retraining, and generalizing across agent models.

</details>


### [77] [Learning from VAE Errors to support ECG-based Differential Diagnosis of Myocardial Scar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05294)
*Shayan Sharifi,Riccardo Treu,Ilaria Gandin,Federico Garoia,Marco Merlo,Giulia Cisotto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于β-变分自编码器（β-VAE）的ECG表征在区分心肌病患者LGE+（心肌疤痕阳性）与LGE-方面的能力，并与基础模型ECGx.AI进行了对比。在300名受试者的本地队列中，ECGx.AI结合随机森林达到AUC 0.686，而所提出的β-VAE达到AUC 0.577（敏感性0.775）；值得注意的是，基于动态时间规整（DTW）的重构误差在12个导联中的10个导联上呈现显著的组间差异，并通过逻辑回归达到AUC 0.643，彰显了其作为疤痕相关ECG改变的标记潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏磁共振的晚期钆增强（LGE）是心肌疤痕的关键标志，但其可及性有限，因此需要基于常规心电图（ECG）的无创筛查方法。本研究旨在探索利用自监督或生成式模型从ECG中提取表征来替代或辅助LGE检测。

Method: 研究在300名心肌病患者的本地队列中，比较了来自基础ECG模型ECGx.AI的32维特征与在正常PTB-XL ECG上训练的较浅β-VAE所提取的特征。评估方式包括下游分类任务以及基于动态时间规整（DTW）的重构误差分析。

Result: ECGx.AI结合随机森林达到AUC（ROC）0.686；所提出的β-VAE结合梯度提升达到AUC 0.577与敏感性0.775。DTW重构误差在12个导联中的10个导联上显示显著的LGE+与LGE-组间差异（Mann-Whitney U检验），且结合逻辑回归后达到AUC 0.643。

Conclusion: DTW重构误差具备作为疤痕相关ECG改变生物标记的潜力，能够辅助LGE的ECG筛查；尽管ECGx.AI在分类性能上更优，但β-VAE的DTW重构误差提供了互补且有意义的判别信息。

Abstract: Late Gadolinium Enhancement (LGE) on cardiac magnetic resonance is a key marker of myocardial scar, but its limited accessibility motivates routine ECG-based screening. We evaluated whether $β$-variational autoencoder (VAE)-derived ECG representations can discriminate LGE+ from LGE- cardiomyopathic patients in a local cohort of 300 subjects. We compared 32-dimensional features from the foundation ECGx.AI model with those from a shallower $β$-VAE trained on normal PTB-XL ECGs, evaluating downstream classification and Dynamic Time Warping (DTW)-based reconstruction errors. ECGx.AI reached an area under ROC of 0.686 with Random Forest, while the proposed $β$-VAE reached 0.577 with sensitivity of 0.775 with Gradient Boosting. Notably, DTW-reconstruction errors significantly differed between classes in 10 out of 12 leads according to Mann-Whitney U test and help in classification, leading to an area under ROC of 0.643 with Logistic Regression, supporting their potential as markers of scar-related ECG alterations.

</details>


### [78] [How Does mHC Use Its Residual Streams? Selective Routing and Near-Identity Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05309)
*Pengxiang Zhao,Xing Li,Xianzhi Yu,Wei Guo,Zhenhua Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了DeepSeek-V4-Flash模型中四流mHC残差通路的使用情况，发现读写路由集中在少数流上且随深度变化，残差混合主要发生在浅层，深层几乎各流独立。干预实验表明晚期混合器功能作用甚微，模型实际只利用了四流mHC提供的一部分灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyper-Connections及其流形约束变体mHC将残差通路从单流扩展为n流，但训练后的模型如何实际利用这种容量仍不清楚，包括块级读写广度、残差通路跨流混合强度以及各流是否携带不同表征。

Method: 使用有效流数、跨流残差权重和流间余弦相似度三类指标，对DeepSeek-V4-Flash的四流残差通路进行分析。同时设计针对性干预实验：替换晚期和早期混合器为恒等映射、将早期混合器固定为C4诊断均值、以及在每个位置仅保留top-3路由权重，以检验各模式的功能意义。

Result: 注意力或FFN位点平均有效使用约两流，主导流随层变化且表征方向性不同。残差混合主要发生在浅层，22-42层各流基本独立传输。将晚期混合器替换为恒等映射仅使C4困惑度增加1.9%并保持六任务平均分不变；替换早期混合器则使困惑度增加41%。将早期混合器固定为其诊断均值仅增加0.2%困惑度；每token保留最大三个路由权重使困惑度增加最多2.7%。

Conclusion: 该模型仅实现了四流mHC所提供的一部分灵活性：单个块很少需要全部四个流，晚期的残差混合几乎没有带来可测量的增益，早期混合器的位点特定结构比其逐token变化更重要。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections and their manifold-constrained variant mHC widen a residual pathway from one stream to n, yet how trained models use this capacity remains unclear: how broadly blocks read and write, how strongly the residual pathway mixes streams, and whether the streams carry distinct representations. We examine these properties in the four-stream residual pathway of DeepSeek-V4-Flash using effective stream counts, cross-stream residual weights, and inter-stream cosine similarity. Read/write routing is concentrated but varies across depth: a typical attention or FFN site effectively uses about two streams, while the dominant stream changes across layers and the representations remain directionally distinct. Residual mixing is modest and occurs primarily in early layers; in layers 22-42, the pathway mostly carries each stream forward separately. Targeted interventions establish the functional significance of these patterns. Replacing the late mixers by identity increases C4 perplexity by only 1.9% and preserves the six-task average score, whereas replacing the early mixers increases perplexity by 41%. Fixing each early mixer to its C4 diagnostic mean increases perplexity by only 0.2% and reduces the average score by 0.25 percentage points, showing that its site-specific structure matters more than its token-wise variation on the evaluated metrics. Likewise, retaining the three largest routing weights per token at every site increases perplexity by at most 2.7% and changes the average score by at most 0.4 points. Thus, the studied model realizes only part of the flexibility afforded by four-stream mHC: individual blocks rarely require all four streams, and late residual mixing provides little measured benefit.

</details>


### [79] [Optimal Rates for Agentic Networked Information Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05318)
*MohammadHossein Bateni,Zahra Hadizadeh,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Shayan Taherijam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了网络化学习模型中的信息聚合问题，解决了Kearns等人（SODA'26）遗留的误差界间隙，证明M-覆盖深度为D的路径上过剩误差率在深度M²之前为常数，之后为Θ(M²/D)，并将相同最优速率推广到逻辑回归分类模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kearns, Roth和Ryu在SODA'26首次提出了网络化学习模型，分析了M-覆盖路径上最后智能体的过剩MSE，给出上界O(M/√D)和下界Ω(M/D)，但两者之间存在显著间隙。闭合这一间隙对理解智能体网络中的信息聚合极限至关重要。

Method: 1) 对循环实例给出更精细的分析，将下界改进为D<M²时的Ω(√(M/D))；2) 对每个D≥M²构造M-覆盖路径，证明过剩误差下界为Ω(M²/D)；3) 证明对任意固定分布，过剩误差沿路径几何收敛，排除单一实例在所有深度同时达到多项式下界的可能性；4) 将回归结果迁移到逻辑分类的logit传递模型，通过证明在这些示例上逻辑路径与最小二乘路径在重新缩放后一致。

Result: 闭合了Kearns等人的间隙：正确速率为深度不超过M²时为常数，超过后为Θ(M²/D)。改进了循环实例下界至Ω(√(M/D))，构造了任意深度D≥M²的下界实例，并证明过剩误差沿路径几何收缩。同时将相同最优速率推广到BCE损失的逻辑分类模型。

Conclusion: 网络化学习中的信息聚合误差率在回归和分类问题中均被完全刻画为Θ(M²/D)（超出深度M²后），在此之前为常数。这为理解agentic AI中分布式预测的精度极限提供了精确的理论基础。

Abstract: Building on the pioneering paper of Kearns, Roth, and Ryu (SODA'26), we study information aggregation in a networked learning model. The model captures a central pattern in agentic AI: each agent sees only part of the data and passes on only its own conclusion. Their model considers a linear regression problem with the mean squared error (MSE) loss. Agents sit in a DAG and each sees only a subset of the features and its parents' predictions, fits a linear predictor, and passes only its prediction forward. The benchmark is the full-feature learner that sees all raw features. A path of depth $D$ is $M$-covered if every block of $M$ consecutive agents collectively sees all raw features. Kearns, Roth, and Ryu proved that the excess mean squared error of the last agent on such a path is $O(M/\sqrt D)$, and gave a cyclic instance with excess error $Ω(M/D)$ for $D<M^2$.
  We close this gap: the correct rate is constant up to depth $M^2$, and $Θ(M^2/D)$ beyond it. We first give a sharper analysis of the cyclic instance and improve its lower bound to $Ω(\sqrt{M/D})$ for $D<M^2$. We then construct, for every depth $D\ge M^2$, an $M$-covered path of depth $D$ with excess error $Ω(M^2/D)$. The same instance gives the constant lower bound for all $D < M^2$. We also show that for any fixed distribution the excess error contracts geometrically along the path, ruling out any single instance that witnesses any polynomial lower bound at every depth.
  Finally, we prove the same optimal rate for logistic classification in the logit-passing model of Bateni et al., which considers the binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss. The same improved upper bound of $O(M^2/D)$ holds, and we transfer all the regression lower bounds by showing that on those examples the logistic path follows the least-squares path up to rescaling.

</details>


### [80] [Embedded Graph Flows for Categorical Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05328)
*Ethan Ma,Zihan Wang,Chris Siu Yeung Chow,Xinguo Feng,Qingqing Li,Rui Jiang,Naipeng Dong,Guangdong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出嵌入式图流（EGF），一种用于分类图生成的生成模型，通过学习节点和边类别的连续嵌入，并利用排列等变图变换器将高斯噪声传输至学习到的端点，最终通过读出层将嵌入映射回离散图类别。在QM9基准上，EGF在所有四项指标上均优于现有方法，FCD达0.150（对比DiGress的0.717和GruM的0.812）；在ZINC250k大分子上，其NSPDK-MMD指标也最低，表明生成的局部子结构与参考分子高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图生成器将类别编码为固定的one-hot向量，这会导致类别之间被强加人为的等距几何结构，限制了模型捕捉类别间自然关系和生成连贯图结构的能力，因此需要一种能学习灵活类别表示并在无序边条件下生成连贯结构化图的模型。

Method: EGF方法分两部分：(1) 为节点和边类别学习连续嵌入端点；(2) 利用排列等变图变换器，将高斯噪声向学习到的类别嵌入端点传输（即嵌入流），最后通过终端的读出层将嵌入映射回离散图类别。该方法将图生成视为从噪声分布到嵌入分布的流匹配问题，有效避免了one-hot编码的人工等距几何限制。

Result: 在QM9基准上，EGF在所有四项指标上取得最佳结果，其中FCD为0.150，显著优于category扩散基线DiGress（0.717）和桥接基线GruM（0.812）。在更大的ZINC250k分子数据集上，EGF在NSPDK-MMD指标上取得最低值，表明生成的分子在局部子结构上与参考分子分布高度吻合。

Conclusion: EGF通过学习类别嵌入而非使用固定的one-hot编码，有效提升了分类图生成的质量，在分子生成基准上展现出明显优势，证明了嵌入流方法在离散图生成任务中的有效性和普适性。

Abstract: Generating categorical graphs requires choosing node and edge types that form a coherent structure without depending on node order. Many graph generators encode categories as fixed one-hot vectors, which can impose an artificial geometry in which categories are equidistant. We propose Embedded Graph Flows (EGF), a generative model that learns continuous embeddings for node and unordered-edge categories and transports Gaussian noise towards these learnt endpoints using a permutation-equivariant graph transformer. A terminal readout maps the embeddings back to discrete graph categories. Across molecular benchmarks, EGF achieved competitive performance. On QM9, EGF gives the best result on all four reported metrics among the three methods, including a Fréchet ChemNet Distance (FCD) of 0.150, compared with 0.717 for the categorical-diffusion baseline DiGress and 0.812 for the bridge-based baseline GruM. When applied to larger molecules in ZINC250k, EGF retains the lowest maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) using the neighbourhood subgraph pairwise distance kernel (NSPDK), indicating close agreement with the local substructures of the reference molecules. Our code is available at https://github.com/Trusted-System-Lab/EGF.

</details>


### [81] [Variational Continuation for Double Pendulum Periodic Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05337)
*Leo Yao,Ziming Liu,Max Tegmark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Hessian矩阵的数值延拓方法，用于在动力系统中寻找周期轨道。该方法将周期轨道参数化为傅里叶级数，利用自动微分（而非手工推导的雅可比矩阵）自动化计算损失函数，通过损失景观的平坦方向（零特征值方向）来确定延拓方向。该方法无需积分器，可精确初始化不稳定不动点附近的振荡，并高效检测轨道族交点和次谐波分岔。作者以双摆系统为例，从不动点出发完成了周期轨道的完整延拓，发现了分岔行为并分类了周期轨道分支，特别找到了两个摆锤从未同时静止的周期轨道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的周期轨道数值延拓方法依赖于手工推导的雅可比矩阵，过程繁琐且易于出错；同时现有方法在初始化不稳定不动点附近的振荡和检测轨道族分岔方面存在局限性。作者希望通过机器学习的自动微分技术自动化这一过程，并利用Hessian矩阵的零特征方向来引导延拓，提高周期轨道搜索的效率和自动化程度。

Method: 1) 将周期轨道（环）参数化为傅里叶级数展开；2) 定义基于轨道偏离物理微分方程程度的损失函数；3) 利用自动微分技术（而非手工推导雅可比）自动计算梯度与Hessian矩阵；4) 通过Hessian矩阵的零特征值（平坦方向）确定延拓方向；5) 采用无积分器的方法，能够从不稳定不动点精确初始化周期振荡。

Result: 以双摆系统为演示，成功完成了从固定点出发的完整周期轨道延拓，展示了沿轨道族的分岔行为并对周期轨道分支进行了分类。特别地，发现了两个摆锤从未同时静止的周期轨道，据作者所知这在以往文献中尚未被记录。

Conclusion: 基于Hessian的自动微分方法为周期轨道数值延拓提供了一种高效、自动化且无需积分器的新途径，能够精确初始化不稳定动力学并在复杂动力系统中发现新的周期轨道类别，拓展了动力系统数值分析的能力。

Abstract: We present a Hessian-based approach to numerically continue periodic orbits in dynamical systems. A loop (periodic orbit candidate) is parametrized as a Fourier series; a loss function is defined based on the deviation of the loop from the physical differential equations. Unlike previous work relying on hand-derived Jacobians, our method automates the process by leveraging automatic differentiation, a common machine learning technique. The continuation direction can be determined by the flat directions of the loss landscapes (directions with zero eigenvalues), making the search of periodic orbits efficient and guided. Our method is integrator-free, precisely initializes oscillations around unstable fixed points, and efficiently detects orbit family intersections and subharmonic bifurcations. As a demonstration, we present full continuations of periodic double pendulum oscillations from fixed points, showing bifurcations along orbit families and categorizing branches of periodic orbits. In particular, we find periodic orbits where both pendulum masses are never simultaneously at rest, which to our knowledge has been missing in the literature.

</details>


### [82] [Distill Globally, Adapt Locally: Reasoning Distillation and Product-Type Test-Time Training for Scalable Trade-Up Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05363)
*Siliang Liu,Mohammad Ghasemi,Sapan Patel,Amin Banitalebi-Dehkordi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两层框架，将大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力蒸馏为高效的非生成式学生模型，用于升级推荐（trade-up recommendation）。第一层通过检索增强的少样本LLM教师生成结构化标签和自然语言理由，监督一个紧凑的嵌入对分类器；第二层通过产品类型测试时训练（PT-TTT）优化类别特定适配器。最终学生模型在推理时仅需两个768维产品嵌入，无需LLM调用，在8,352对人类标注基准上达到AUC 0.941，且比直接LLM推理快约5,000倍、成本低约10,000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 升级推荐需要识别在保持购买意图的同时提供更高品质替代的产品。虽然LLM能够推理这种区分，但直接应用于数亿产品对在计算上不可行，因此需要一种能够将LLM推理能力高效部署到大规模场景的方法。

Method: 提出两层框架：Level 1使用检索增强的少样本LLM教师生成结构化关系标签与自然语言理由，通过对齐和对比目标监督紧凑的嵌入对分类器（15.5M参数、四分类、推理时仅用两个768维嵌入）；Level 2引入产品类型测试时训练（PT-TTT），利用少样本演示在冻结学生模型上优化轻量级类别特定适配器。

Result: 在8,352对人类标注基准上，推理蒸馏学生模型AUC达0.924，优于仅标签学生模型的0.912；PT-TTT进一步提升AUC至0.941、平均精度至0.940。在100K对代理目录上，单台八GPU机器上推理速度约为直接LLM推理的5,000倍，估计成本降低约10,000倍。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将LLM的高级推理能力通过蒸馏和适配器优化的方式转化为高效的轻量级部署方案，在保持高准确率的同时实现了大规模升级推荐的实际可行性，验证了推理蒸馏与测试时训练相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Trade-up recommendation identifies higher-quality alternatives that preserve a customer's purchase intent while offering upgraded benefits. Large language models (LLMs) can reason about such distinctions, but applying them directly to hundreds of millions of product pairs is operationally impractical. We introduce a two-level framework that distills LLM reasoning into an efficient non-generative student and adapts its decision boundary to product-type-specific trade-up criteria. At Level 1, a retrieval-augmented few-shot LLM teacher generates structured relation labels and natural-language rationales. These rationales supervise a compact embedding-pair classifier through alignment and contrastive objectives; at inference, the student uses only two precomputed 768-dimensional product embeddings, with no LLM calls or text generation. On a fixed human-annotated benchmark of 8,352 pairs, a 15.5M-parameter four-class reasoning-distilled student achieves AUC 0.924 (95% CI [0.918, 0.929]), compared with 0.912 for the four-class label-only student. At Level 2, product-type test-time training (PT-TTT) uses few-shot demonstrations to optimize lightweight category-specific adapters over the frozen student. PT-TTT improves AUC from 0.924 to 0.941 and average precision from 0.920 to 0.940. On a 100K-pair proxy catalog, the distilled student on a single eight-GPU machine is approximately 5,000x faster and 10,000x lower in estimated cost than direct LLM inference.

</details>


### [83] [RegionFed: Federated Learning for Personalized Query Understanding in Heterogeneous Retail Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05403)
*Quoc H. Nguyen,Ali Lafzi,Abhijeet Phatak,Siddharth Pratap Singh,Rohit Upadhyay,Yogananda Domlur Seetharama,Chittaranjan Tripathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RegionFed是一个架构鲁棒的联邦学习框架，通过梯度级ℓ2冲突信号实现区域个性化，在T5等现代Transformer上避免了参数级方法的灾难性崩溃，达到了92.27%的准确率，几乎追平中心化上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售搜索系统服务不同地理区域时存在数据异构性，标准FL牺牲区域性能，而现有参数级个性化FL方法在Transformer上因嵌入绑定和LayerNorm交互而崩溃（T5上准确率低于10%）。

Method: RegionFed完全在梯度层面操作，使用区域梯度与全局梯度之间的ℓ2冲突作为统一信号，用于：(i)诊断异构性，(ii)将每个区域路由到最经济的个性化策略，(iii)自适应控制个性化强度。该方法将模型视为可微黑盒，无需修改代码即可部署在多种架构上。

Result: 在三个公开数据集和四种架构上，RegionFed-Meta达到92.27%准确率，与侵犯隐私的中心化上界(92.04%)仅差0.23个百分点（在1σ内），同时提供ε≈0.60的差分隐私和O(1/√T)收敛保证。在Transformer上大幅超越参数级方法，在CNN上也有持续改进。

Conclusion: 梯度级联邦个性化范式能有效规避参数级方法在Transformer架构上的结构性局限，实现架构无关的区域个性化，缩小与中心化训练的性能差距，同时保持隐私保障和理论收敛性。

Abstract: Retail search systems serve diverse geographic regions with distinct query patterns, vocabularies, and product preferences, creating significant data heterogeneity that challenges both privacy-preserving training and model personalization. Federated learning offers a natural solution for privacy, but standard FL methods produce global models that sacrifice regional performance, while existing personalized FL approaches operate at the parameter level and catastrophically collapse on modern transformers (below 10\% accuracy on T5) due to tied embeddings and LayerNorm interactions. We introduce RegionFed, an \textit{architecture-robust} federated learning framework that sidesteps this failure by operating entirely at the gradient level. RegionFed uses the $\ell_2$ conflict between regional and global gradients as a unified signal that (i) diagnoses heterogeneity, (ii) routes each region to the cheapest sufficient personalization strategy, and (iii) adaptively controls personalization strength. Because it treats models as differentiable black boxes, RegionFed deploys on T5-Small, T5-3B, RoBERTa, and CNN with zero code changes, providing large gains on transformers (where parameter-level methods collapse) and consistent improvements on CNNs. Across three public datasets (Amazon ESCI, Amazon Reviews, LEAF-FEMNIST) and four architectures, RegionFed-Meta achieves 92.27\%, closing the gap to the privacy-violating centralized upper bound (Centralized + Regional Weighting: 92.04\%, $Δ$=0.23pp, within 1$σ$) while providing $(ε{\approx}0.60)$-differential privacy and $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ convergence.

</details>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [Embodied Multimedia: A Tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04204)
*Yang Liu,Wei Zuo,Guanwei Zhao,Juncen Guo,Jiangchuan Liu,Abdulmotaleb Saddik,Liang Song*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文正式提出"具身多媒体"（Embodied Multimedia）这一跨学科研究范式，针对传统多媒体技术（面向人类观察者优化）与具身智能体（需要实时感知、推理和行动）之间的根本性不匹配，提出涵盖数据、通信、认知和评估四层统一架构，并回顾了各层关键技术，识别了五大前沿应用方向及未来研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多媒体技术围绕人类观察者优化内容分发（如感知驱动的压缩标准和以人为中心的质量指标），但随着具身智能的迅速崛起，自主智能体需要在物理世界中实时感知、推理和行动，这暴露了传统多媒体基础设施与具身任务需求之间的根本性不匹配，亟需一个新的研究范式来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 本文为教程类论文，采用理论构建与系统综述相结合的方法：首先正式定义Embodied Multimedia范式，将多模态数据视为覆盖完整感知-决策-行动循环的感知与通信基质；其次提出包含数据层、通信层、认知层和评估层的四层统一架构，并结构化综述各层的关键使能技术；最后识别五大前沿应用方向并讨论开放挑战。

Result: 本文构建了Embodied Multimedia的完整技术框架（四层架构）和五大前沿应用方向（多媒体通信、物理智能、具身异常感知、元宇宙与交互式多媒体、AI驱动的艺术创作），并系统梳理了各层关键技术及该领域面临的技术挑战和未来研究方向。

Conclusion: Embodied Multimedia作为连接传统多媒体技术与具身智能需求的新兴研究领域，具有基础性作用。本文通过形式化定义和架构梳理为社区提供了该领域的系统性认知和指引，同时指出仍存在大量开放技术挑战，需要学界和工业界共同努力推动这一领域的成熟与发展。

Abstract: Traditional multimedia technology has been built around optimizing content delivery for human observers, from perceptually driven compression standards to human-centric quality metrics. With the rapid rise of embodied intelligence, autonomous agents must perceive, reason, and act within the physical world in real time, exposing fundamental mismatches between conventional multimedia infrastructure and the demands of embodied tasks. In this regard, this tutorial paper formally introduces Embodied Multimedia as a cross-disciplinary research paradigm that treats multimodal data as the perceptual and communicative substrate spanning the full perception-decision-action loop. To be specific, we present a four-layer unified architecture comprising Data, Communication, Cognitive, and Evaluation layers, and provide a structured review of key enabling technologies within each layer. Furthermore, we identify five frontier application directions where Embodied Multimedia is positioned to serve a foundational role: multimedia communication, physical intelligence, embodied anomaly perception, the metaverse and interactive multimedia, and AI-driven art creation. Open technical challenges and future research directions are discussed to guide the community in this emerging field.

</details>


### [85] [Encore: Infinite Audio-Video Generation with Adaptive Signal Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04249)
*Shaohua Pan,Junbao Chen,Shengyi He,Jingfeng Xue,Wen Tao,Haocheng Feng,Siming Fan,Dongwei Pan,Yi Yang,Wei He,Hang Zhou*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出Encore框架，用于长时程音视频联合生成。其核心将长时程音视频生成挑战分解为局部连续性和全局一致性两个层面，分别通过跨块上下文传播的迭代生成和带偏移位置编码的参考音视频信号来解决。作者进一步提出自适应信号路由（ASR）机制，通过学习注意力偏置和残差缩放来自适应调节各条件信号的影响。实验表明Encore在生成质量和时间一致性上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频生成方法要么只能生成短视频（虽能良好同步但时长受限），要么能生成长视频却完全缺乏音频。长时程音视频联合生成面临独特挑战：每个块需要同时保持视频时间相干性、音频时间相干性以及跨模态同步，而不同条件信号通过不同路径进入模型，远比单一任务更难。

Method: 将长时程音视频生成分解为：(1) 局部连续性—通过迭代生成与显式跨块上下文传播处理；(2) 全局一致性—通过参考音视频信号加偏移位置编码来保证。在此基础上提出自适应信号路由（ASR），在自注意力中引入可学习注意力偏置、在交叉注意力输出上引入可学习残差缩放，使模型能自适应调节每种条件信号的权重。模型还支持推理阶段通过以真实模态为条件实现无限长度的音视频/视频到音频/音频到视频生成。

Result: 在扩展的VerseBench长音视频评估基准上，Encore在生成质量与时间一致性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Encore通过因子化解耦局部连续性与全局一致性，并配合自适应信号路由机制，有效解决了长时程音视频联合生成的难题，为后续长音视频生成研究提供了新的思路和开源代码/数据资源。

Abstract: Existing audio-video generation methods produce well-synchronized clips but are limited to short durations, while long-video generation methods extend duration through chunk-based iterative synthesis yet lack audio entirely. Generating long audio-video jointly is fundamentally harder than either task alone: each chunk must simultaneously maintain video temporal coherence, audio temporal coherence, and cross-modal synchronization, whose conditioning signals enter the model through different pathways. In this work, we present Encore for long-form synchronized audio-video generation. Our key insight is to factor this challenge into: (1) local continuity handled by iterative generation with explicit cross-chunk context propagation, and (2) global consistency enforced via reference audio-video signals with shifted position embedding. Building on this design, we propose Adaptive Signal Routing (ASR), which introduces learnable attention biases within self-attention and learnable residual scales on cross-attention outputs, enabling the model to adaptively modulate the influence of each conditioning signal. Trained end-to-end for joint audio-video generation, Encore also supports infinite-length audio-to-video and video-to-audio synthesis at inference by conditioning on the ground-truth modality throughout the denoising process. Experiments on our extended VerseBench for long audio-video evaluation demonstrate that Encore significantly outperforms existing methods in both generation quality and temporal coherence. Code and data for this paper are at https://github.com/shaohua-pan/Encore.

</details>


### [86] [AVENUE: Audio-Video EditiNg Understanding and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04253)
*Hayeon Kim,Yoojin Jang,Jaejun Yoo*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出AVENUE，一个面向音视频编辑的多模态理解和评估基准，包含1,291个源片段和7,957条编辑指令，覆盖音视频定位和联合编辑等类型，并引入样本特定、模态感知的评估框架，系统分析联合、序列和分离三种编辑范式的模态选择性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频编辑基准对编辑类型和模态组合覆盖有限，评估系统往往模态盲且样本无关，难以判断模型是否忠实保留了非目标模态，缺乏对模态选择性编辑能力的系统评估。

Method: 构建AVENUE基准（基于VGGSound人工审核的1,291个片段和7,957条指令），涵盖音频目标、视频目标和音视频耦合编辑类型；提出样本特定且模态感知的评估框架，为每个样本明确指定预期变更内容和需保持不变的模态内容，并评估联合、序列和分离三种编辑范式的代表性模型。

Result: 发现一个根本性开放挑战：无论采用何种编辑范式，编辑某一模态时现有模型经常在另一模态中引发非预期的改变。该研究提供了首个跨范式的模态选择性系统分析。

Conclusion: AVENUE为更可控的音视频编辑模型提供了基准和模态感知评估框架，数据集已公开于Hugging Face，旨在推动音视频编辑领域的发展。

Abstract: Audio-video (AV) editing aims to modify audio and video content according to a target prompt. Unlike single-modality editing, AV editing requires models to infer a modality-selective edit scope from the prompt alone: determining not only what should change, but also which modality should be preserved. Faithfully evaluating such models therefore requires both (i) benchmarks that span diverse edit types and modality categories, and (ii) evaluation that is itself modality-aware and sample-specific. However, existing AV editing benchmarks provide limited coverage of edit types and modality combinations, while current evaluation systems are often modality-blind and sample-agnostic, making it difficult to assess whether models faithfully preserve the unintended modality. To address these gaps, we introduce AVENUE, Audio-Video EditiNg Understanding and Evaluation, comprising two contributions: (1) a benchmark of 1,291 source clips and 7,957 editing instructions across audio-targeted, video-targeted, and AV-coupled edit types, curated and human-verified from VGGSound; and (2) a sample-specific, modality-aware evaluation framework that specifies, for each sample, both the intended change and the content that must remain intact. We evaluate representative AV editing models spanning three editing paradigms : joint, sequential, and separate, providing the first systematic analysis of modality-selectivity across paradigms. Our findings reveal a fundamental open challenge: when editing one modality, existing models frequently induce unintended changes in the other, regardless of paradigm. AVENUE provides a benchmark and modality-aware evaluation framework to drive progress toward more controllable AV editing models. Our dataset is publicly available on Hugging Face: https://huggingface.co/datasets/AVENUE-dataset/AVENUE.

</details>


### [87] [PRISM-Bench: An Audio-Centric Diagnostic Benchmark for Text-to-Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04867)
*Yuchen Sun,Qian Yang,Jun Wang,Detai Xin,Guoqiao Yu,Guanglu Wan,Qi Jia*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM-Bench，首个以音频为中心的文本到音视频（T2AV）生成诊断基准，包含900个人工验证样本，沿音频类型（语音/音乐/声音）和声源可见性（画面内/外）两个正交轴分解评估，从四个感知维度共35项细粒度标准评测生成内容，并采用增强型MLLM裁判协议实现与人类评估者超过70%的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2AV生成评估方法对音频模态的关注不足：要么将音频视为视频质量的辅助部分，要么脱离视听关联单独评估音频，导致难以诊断系统在音频生成上的真实优劣。

Method: 构建包含900个人工验证样本的PRISM-Bench数据集，沿音频类型（语音、音乐、声音）和声源可见性（画面内/外）两个正交轴进行因子化评估；通过四个感知维度（视听一致性、音频质量、音频表现力、提示跟随）下35项细粒度标准进行评测；采用基于盲测侧对与真实参照比较的增强MLLM-as-a-Judge协议来保证评估可靠性。

Result: 评测展示了前沿与开源T2AV模型之间的显著性能差距；发现当前生成范式过度侧重于感知保真度，但在复杂接地与控制任务上表现不足，尤其在音乐生成和画面内同步音频生成方面存在困难。

Conclusion: PRISM-Bench为T2AV音频生成提供了更全面、细粒度的诊断工具，揭示了现有系统在复杂音视频接地任务上的短板，为后续改进指明方向。裁判协议与人类评估超70%的一致性验证了其评估可靠性。

Abstract: Text-to-audio-video (T2AV) generation has advanced rapidly, but its evaluation still underestimates the audio modality. Existing benchmarks either treat audio as an auxiliary component of video quality or assess it in isolation from audiovisual grounding, making it difficult to diagnose where current systems truly succeed or fail in audio generation. We present PRISM-Bench, the first audio-centric diagnostic benchmark for T2AV generation. Built from a rigorously curated dataset of 900 human-verified samples, PRISM-Bench factorizes audio evaluation along two orthogonal axes: audio type (Speech, Music, and Sound) and sound-source visibility (On-screen vs. Off-screen). It evaluates generated content across four perceptual dimensions (Audio-Visual Coherence, Audio Quality, Audio Expressiveness, and Prompt Following) with 35 fine-grained criteria. To ensure reliable assessment, we adopt an enhanced MLLM-as-a-Judge protocol based on blind, side-by-side comparison against ground-truth references, demonstrating strong alignment (over 70% mean agreement) with human raters. Our evaluation of recent T2AV systems highlights a significant performance gap between frontier and open-source models. Furthermore, we demonstrate that current generation paradigms overfit to perceptual fidelity while struggling with complex grounding and control tasks, particularly in generating music and synchronized On-screen audio.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [SAGE: Semantic Attribute Graphs for Multi-Entity Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04255)
*Yongjoo Kim,Mincheol Kwon,Seonga Choi,Minseung Lee,Kyeong-Jin Oh,Hyunyoung Lee,Yunsu Choi,Jungbeom Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE框架和DEAR数据集，解决密集文档图像中细粒度实体的"语义稀释"问题，通过无训练的层次化图结构与多向量嵌入实现实体级检索，在Recall@3达0.849。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集文档图像中细粒度视觉和文本实体的相关性取决于用户查询，但标准视觉-语言检索器用单一向量编码裁剪区域，可能混合不同实体信号并掩盖细粒度检索所需证据，即"语义稀释"问题。

Method: 提出SAGE（无训练框架）：从密集文档图像中解析语义实体，将其表示为带多向量嵌入的层次化图节点，通过迭代的实体级子图匹配检索与查询相关的证据；同时引入DEAR数据集（1,055对查询-图像对），涵盖四种复杂度递增的问题类型。

Result: SAGE显著减少语义稀释，在DEAR上优于基于patch级别和OCR的检索基线，多实体视觉比较查询的Recall@3达0.849，生成分数2.746。

Conclusion: SAGE通过层次化图结构和多向量嵌入有效缓解了密集文档图像中的语义稀释问题，为细粒度实体级检索提供了可行的无训练解决方案。

Abstract: Dense document images often contain many fine-grained visual and textual entities whose relevance depends on a user query. Standard vision-language retrievers encode cropped regions with a single vector, which can mix distinct entity signals and obscure the evidence needed for fine-grained retrieval. We call this failure mode Semantic Dilution and quantitatively show that it degrades entity-level retrieval as a function of entity density. To mitigate it, we propose SAGE, a training-free framework that parses semantic entities from dense document images, represents them as hierarchical graph nodes with multi-vector embeddings, and retrieves query-relevant evidence through iterative entity-level subgraph matching. We also introduce DEAR, a dataset of 1,055 query--image pairs sourced from product detail pages, where each query requires retrieving and comparing multiple fine-grained entities from visually dense inputs across four question types of increasing complexity. Experiments show that SAGE substantially reduces semantic dilution and outperforms patch-level and OCR-based retrieval baselines on DEAR, achieving a Recall@3 of 0.849 and a generation score of 2.746 on multi-entity visual comparison queries. Our code is available at https://github.com/All4Nothing/SAGE.

</details>


### [89] [MURAL: Multimodal Uncertainty-aware Recommendation via Adaptive edge Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04574)
*Ahmad Mousavi,Majid Alikhani,Yeon-Chang Lee,Roberto Corizzo,Yeganeh Abdollahinejad*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MURAL提出多模态图神经网络推荐统一框架，通过自适应边学习解决结构刚性，通过不确定性感知融合解决语义脆弱性，在大规模基准上超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态图神经网络推荐系统面临两大瓶颈：一是依赖静态预计算相似图导致结构刚性，无法适应动态演化的用户偏好；二是多模态信号被不加区分地融合，噪声信号扭曲了协同信号，导致语义脆弱。

Method: MURAL包含两大核心模块：(1) 自适应边学习器，结合可微分检索增强策略与近似最近邻搜索，实现O(NlogN)复杂度下动态发现潜在物品-物品相关性；(2) 不确定性感知融合模块，建模异质模态的偶然不确定性，动态降低不可靠特征权重同时优先高置信度信号；(3) 对比师生对齐策略，将模态特定表示锚定到稳定的行为信号，保证优化稳定性且无梯度泄漏。

Result: 在TikTok和Amazon等大规模基准上，MURAL显著超越结构和生成式SOTA基线，在准确性上表现优越，同时通过领域特定模态主导性提供可解释性，并在极端数据损坏下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MURAL展示了将多模态推荐从固定的结构增强转向动态拓扑发现的有效性，通过不确定性感知融合和对比对齐策略同时解决了结构刚性与语义脆弱性问题，为多模态推荐提供了兼顾性能、可解释性和鲁棒性的新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal Graph Neural Networks have become standard for recommendation by augmenting sparse interaction data with content features. Yet current architectures face two bottlenecks: structural rigidity, from a reliance on static precomputed similarity graphs that cannot adapt to evolving preferences; and semantic fragility, where noisy modality signals are indiscriminately fused, distorting the collaborative signal. We propose MURAL (Multimodal Uncertainty-aware Recommendation via Adaptive edge Learning), a unified framework that shifts multimodal recommendation from fixed structural augmentation to dynamic topology discovery. To address structural rigidity, an Adaptive Edge Learner combines a differentiable retrieval-augmented strategy with an approximate nearest neighbor search to discover latent item-item correlations that are both semantically adaptive and computationally scalable (O(NlogN)). To address semantic fragility, an Uncertainty-Aware Fusion module models the aleatoric uncertainty of heterogeneous modalities, dynamically down-weighting unreliable features while prioritizing high-confidence signals as a defense against cross-modal noise. We further employ a contrastive teacher-student alignment that anchors modality-specific representations to stable behavioral signals, ensuring optimization stability without gradient leakage. Experiments on large-scale benchmarks including TikTok and Amazon show that MURAL significantly surpasses both structural and generative state-of-the-art baselines, achieving superior accuracy while offering interpretability through domain-specific modality dominance and robustness under extreme data corruption.

</details>


### [90] [Latent-Aligned Reasoning for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04645)
*Jiarui Jin,Anyang Ji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LARK框架，通过两阶段潜在推理机制解决多模态推荐中视觉与文本信号在推理过程中逐渐衰减（即跨模态稀释）的问题，在多个公开基准和工业数据集上取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态视觉-语言模型在跨模态理解上表现优异，但当应用于推荐任务时，随着多步推理的进行，视觉和文本信号会逐步衰减（被称为跨模态稀释现象），这一问题尚无有效解决方案。

Method: LARK采用两阶段推理框架：第一阶段，在单VLM中交织可学习潜在token与多步链式思维推理，并与冻结的视觉编码器显式对齐，作为保留感知细节的视觉检查点；第二阶段，通过桥接MLP投影潜在表示并进行item间对比学习，同时将中间特征与第一阶段CoT的隐藏状态对齐，防止推理语义衰减。

Result: 在三个公开基准和一个工业数据集上的实验表明，LARK在多种推荐架构上均达到最先进性能，并通过控制消融实验确认了各组件各自独立的贡献。

Conclusion: LARK通过互补的对齐机制有效缓解了跨模态稀释问题，验证了潜在对齐推理策略在多模态推荐中的有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in cross-modal understanding, yet a fundamental challenge persists when applying them to recommendation: as representations propagate through multi-step reasoning, both visual and textual signals progressively attenuate - a phenomenon we term cross-modal dilution. To address this, we propose LARK (Latent-Aligned Reasoning frameworK), a two-stage latent reasoning framework with complementary alignment mechanisms within a single VLM. In the first stage, learnable latent tokens are interleaved with multi-step chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and explicitly aligned with a frozen vision encoder, serving as visual checkpoints that preserve perceptual details throughout the reasoning chain. In the second stage, the latent representations are projected via a bridge MLP and trained with item-to-item contrastive learning; to prevent the reasoning semantics from fading, intermediate features are aligned with the CoT hidden states from the first stage, anchoring the final embeddings to the model's own reasoning output. Experiments on three public benchmarks and one industrial dataset show that LARK achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple recommendation architectures, with controlled ablations confirming the distinct contribution of each component.

</details>


### [91] [VizIt: A multi-view framework for exploring single-cell, spatial, and genetic data online](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04658)
*Chenhang Christopher Zhang,Yanqing Lou,Jie Yuan,Mingming Lu,Jacob Parker,Himanshu Chintalapudi,Zechuan Lin,Clemens R. Scherzer,Yuxuan Hu,Ruifeng Hu,Xianjun Dong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VizIt是一个开源框架，用于单细胞和空间转录组学、表观基因组学及遗传学数据的多视角交互式探索，并已通过帕金森细胞图谱进行了演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组学研究需要从互补的生物学视角审视数据，但现有的交互式探索工具在多模态和多工具之间仍然碎片化，缺乏统一的探索框架。

Method: 提出了VizIt开源框架，将基因、细胞类型、条件、空间、基因组区域和变异等六种中心化视图相连接，实现跨生物学视角的无缝导航。

Result: 通过帕金森细胞图谱这一可定制化交互式多组学资源进行了演示验证。

Conclusion: VizIt提供了一个统一的多视角探索框架，能够有效整合不同类型组学数据，促进跨视角的生物学发现。

Abstract: Multi-omic studies increasingly require data to be examined from complementary biological perspectives, yet interactive exploration remains fragmented across modalities and tools. We present VizIt, an open-source framework for multi-view exploration of single-cell and spatial transcriptomic, epigenomic and genetic data. VizIt connects gene-, cell type-, condition-, spatial-, genomic region- and variant-centered views, enabling seamless navigation across biological perspectives. We demonstrate VizIt through the Parkinson's Cell Atlas, a customizable interactive multi-omic resource.

</details>


### [92] [Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization for Training-Free AI Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04813)
*Wei Zhou,Tiandeng Wu,Jiandong Ding,Zhufeng Fan,Yi Cao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出IGPO（Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization），一种面向固定AI搜索管道的免训练优化方法。它通过将策略与库存环境事实分离，在线实时探测库存并构造库存画像，离线进行随机回放以获取对比信号，实现无需微调或强化学习即可提升AI搜索系统性能。在商业智能助手AI搜索系统中上线后，14天A/B测试显示CTR相对提升3.17%，审计不良案例减少38.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI搜索系统早期部署时面临产品目录频繁更新的问题，库存及商品属性不可视为稳定知识。传统微调、强化学习和静态提示补丁等方法均不适用：标签稀缺、奖励随库存漂移、模型发布成本高、提示修复易过时。因此需要一种不依赖稳定库存知识、无需训练即可适应动态环境的优化方法。

Method: IGPO将策略与库存环境事实解耦，包含两个核心组件：（1）离线阶段：按查询对随机回放分组，混合结果组直接产生对比信号；库存引导的探索循环可区分检索遗漏和库存证据下无匹配支持两种情况，从而学习策略准则（Policy Guidelines）。（2）在线阶段：探测库存并构建库存画像，将与当前查询相关的策略准则注入检索和选择提示中。

Result: 自2026年5月起在商业智能助手AI搜索系统中部署。14天在线A/B测试结果显示，完整的IGPO处理带来3.17%的相对CTR提升，审计的不良案例减少38.9%。

Conclusion: IGPO为动态库存环境下的AI搜索优化提供了一种免训练、可部署的实用方案，通过环境感知的策略准则而非记忆商品内容，有效适应商品目录频繁更新的场景，实验证明了其在实际系统中的显著效果。

Abstract: Early in deployment, an AI search system typically operates over a frequently updated product catalog, so the available items and their properties cannot be treated as stable knowledge that can be encoded in fixed prompts or strategies. Fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and static prompt patches fit poorly: labels are scarce, rewards drift with inventory, model releases are costly, and prompt fixes quickly stale. We present Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization (IGPO), a training-free approach for fixed AI search pipelines. IGPO separates policy from environment facts: it learns Policy Guidelines for acting on runtime inventory evidence rather than memorizing available items. Online, IGPO grounds each query by probing the inventory and constructing an inventory portrait, then injects relevant Policy Guidelines into the retrieval and selection prompts. Offline, stochastic rollouts are grouped by query -- mixed outcome groups directly yield contrastive signal, and an inventory-guided exploration loop distinguishes missed retrieval routes from cases where no matching support is found under the observed inventory evidence. Since May 2026, IGPO has been deployed in a commercial smart-assistant AI search system. A 14-day online A/B test of the complete IGPO treatment shows a 3.17% relative CTR lift and a 38.9% reduction in audited bad cases.

</details>


### [93] [Personalized Task Dependency Graphs for Mitigating Signal Erosion in Multi-Task Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04862)
*Fuyuan Liu,Tiandeng Wu,Yaqun Fang,Wei Zhou,Zehao Zhou,Wenping Chen,Qishun Mei,Jiaxin Zhou,Heng Chang,Yi Cao,Jiandong Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PTDG方法，通过低秩近似动态调整商品间的任务依赖路径强度，并结合GCN传播与自适应渐进掩码策略，解决工业推荐多任务学习中的信号衰减问题，在稀疏深度漏斗目标上显著提升AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTL方法在多目标推荐中通常沿固定转换漏斗施加统一依赖强度，忽视了任务相关性随商品特征自然变化的特性；层级消息传递沿固定链路累积衰减，导致稀疏深层漏斗目标性能退化。

Method: PTDG在遵守物理因果约束（如点击->支付）的前提下，通过低秩逼近为每个商品动态重构依赖路径强度；采用GCN传播配合硬因果掩码建立自适应信息捷径；引入自适应渐进掩码（APM）策略，按任务稀疏程度解耦共享参数以稳定优化。

Result: 在KuaiRand1K及工业数据集上，PTDG在稀疏转化任务上AUC最高提升1.45%，稠密目标性能保持相当；线上A/B测试中CVR提升1.2%，eCPM提升1.9%。

Conclusion: PTDG通过个性化任务依赖图与自适应渐进掩码策略，有效缓解了固定层级架构中的信号衰减问题，在稀疏深度漏斗目标上取得了显著且稳定的提升，验证了动态调整任务依赖强度的可行性。

Abstract: Optimizing multiple conversion objectives is a core challenge in industrial recommendation, often limited by signal erosion in rigid architectures. Existing Multi-Task Learning (MTL) methods typically enforce uniform dependency strengths across a static conversion funnel, overlooking how task correlations naturally vary based on item characteristics. Hierarchical message passing along these fixed chains leads to cumulative signal attenuation, which degrades performance on sparse, deep-funnel objectives. To address this, we propose the Personalized Task Dependency Graphs (PTDG). While respecting necessary physical causal constraints (e.g., Click -> Pay), PTDG dynamically "rewires" the intensity of dependency pathways for each item via low-rank approximation to ensure structural robustness. We implement a GCN-based propagation with hard causal masking to establish adaptive information shortcuts. Additionally, we introduce an Adaptive Progressive Masking (APM) strategy that decouples shared parameters according to task sparsity, helping to stabilize optimization. Experiments on KuaiRand1K and an industrial dataset show that PTDG significantly improves AUC on sparse conversion tasks by up to 1.45%, while maintaining comparable performance on dense objectives. Online A/B testing shows PTDG improves Conversion Rate (CVR) by 1.2% and effective Cost Per Mille (eCPM) by 1.9% relative to the baseline.

</details>


### [94] [AtomRec: Evolving Atomic Memory for Agentic Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04882)
*Peiyu Hu,Weihai Lu,Siying Gu,Zhuodong Liu,Zhaokai Luo,Yuean Niu,Zhiyong Wang,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出AtomRec，一种基于演化原子协作记忆的智能体推荐系统。它将用户和物品记忆表示为结构化原子单元，构建语义链接，并在新交互到来时演化相关历史记忆。推荐时通过多跳证据路径检索链接记忆，而非孤立的邻居摘要，使协作信号支持可解释的排序。在四个公开基准上，较现有最优基线平均提升约8.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的智能体推荐系统在记忆机制上存在不足：将用户和物品信息压缩为粗粒度摘要，并用标量协作链接连接，难以保留细粒度的偏好阶段，也无法在用户兴趣演化时检索可解释的证据。

Method: 提出AtomRec框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 将用户和物品记忆表示为结构化原子单元；(2) 在相关记忆之间构建语义链接；(3) 当新交互到来时演化相关历史记忆。推荐阶段检索链接记忆作为多跳证据路径，实现证据感知的协作信号支持排序。

Result: 在四个公开基准数据集上，AtomRec一致性地优于现有最先进的智能体和记忆增强基线方法，各项指标平均相对提升约8.5%。

Conclusion: AtomRec通过引入原子化和演化的记忆结构以及多跳证据路径检索，有效解决了现有记忆机制粗粒度、难以追踪偏好演化的问题，为智能体推荐系统提供了一种更精细、更可解释的记忆建模方案。

Abstract: Agentic recommender systems use large language models to maintain semantic memory and support evidence-aware recommendation. However, existing memory mechanisms often compress user and item information into coarse summaries and connect them with scalar collaborative links, making it difficult to preserve fine-grained preference stages or retrieve interpretable evidence as user interests evolve. We propose \textsc{AtomRec}, an agentic recommender with evolving atomic collaborative memory. \textsc{AtomRec} represents user and item memories as structured atomic units, builds semantic links across related memories, and evolves related historical fields when new interactions arrive. During recommendation, it retrieves linked memories as multi-hop evidence paths rather than isolated neighbor summaries, allowing collaborative signals to support grounded ranking. Experiments on four public benchmarks show that \textsc{AtomRec} consistently outperforms state-of-the-art agentic and memory-augmented baselines, with around 8.5\% average relative improvement across metrics.

</details>


### [95] [SAM-D2Q: Aligning Multimodal Doc2Query with Search Demand and Conversion for E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04961)
*Hui Zhou,Jian Hui Ji,Lei Ma,Rong Xiao,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SAM-D2Q框架，一种面向电商搜索的商业对齐多模态文档扩展方法，通过三个阶段的训练（多模态监督微调、数据增强、强化学习偏好对齐），解决传统Doc2Query仅基于文本且不考虑商业目标的问题，在AliExpress生产系统中GMV提升3.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索面临用户查询与商家商品标题之间的词汇不匹配问题，因为短标题无法完全覆盖用户多样的表达方式和商品的视觉属性。传统Doc2Query方法仅基于文本且未针对电商业务目标优化，可能生成语义上合理但商业上无效的扩展内容，同时遗漏商品图片中的关键属性。

Method: SAM-D2Q由三个阶段构成：(1)任务适配的多模态监督微调，增强模型对商品标题、图片和用户查询的视觉-语言理解能力；(2)多模态数据增强，提升对关键视觉属性的感知和扩展覆盖率；(3)基于强化学习的偏好对齐，向搜索业务目标优化，鼓励模型生成更符合用户意图和商业价值的伪查询。

Result: 离线实验表明SAM-D2Q相比传统Doc2Query方法在检索性能上有显著提升。在AliExpress生产搜索系统中部署后，GMV提升+3.38%，支付订单数提升+2.27%。

Conclusion: 多模态文档扩展结合商业目标对齐，能够有效解决电商搜索中文档扩展仅考虑文本语义而忽视商品视觉属性和商业价值的问题，并且在真实生产环境中验证了其商业有效性。

Abstract: E-commerce search often suffers from vocabulary mismatch between user queries and merchant-authored product titles, since short titles cannot fully cover diverse user expressions or visual product attributes. Although Doc2Query alleviates this issue by generating pseudo-queries for document expansion, traditional methods are text-only and not optimized for e-commerce business objectives. As a result, they may produce semantically plausible but commercially ineffective expansions and miss key attributes present in product images. To this end, we propose E-commerce Search-Aligned Multimodal Doc2Query (SAM-D2Q), a business-aligned multimodal document expansion framework for e-commerce search under Boolean retrieval constraints. SAM-D2Q consists of three stages: (1) task-adapted multimodal supervised fine-tuning to enhance vision-language understanding of product titles, images, and user queries; (2) multimodal data augmentation to improve perception of key visual attributes and expansion coverage; and (3) reinforcement-learning-based preference alignment toward search business objectives, encouraging the model to generate pseudo-queries that better match user intent and commercial value. Offline experiments show that SAM-D2Q substantially improves retrieval performance over traditional Doc2Query methods. Deployed in the AliExpress production search system, SAM-D2Q improves online business metrics, increasing GMV by +3.38% and Pay Count by +2.27%.

</details>


### [96] [Repeated Queries Exhaust an LLM's Brand Recommendations but Not Its Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05059)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究发现，无论是否重复相同的品牌推荐问题，语言模型的品牌推荐是否会"穷尽"取决于是否启用检索（retrieval）。无网络搜索的引擎在连续15次运行后仍持续输出新品牌（86-92%的单元仍在新增），而启用检索的引擎则更快趋于饱和。关键词涵盖300个"问题-引擎"单元格、1470个经过裁决的组织实体、Chao2丰富度估计、精确稀疏化等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在重复相同购买问题时，其品牌推荐列表是否会收敛/穷尽，以及这种收敛是否受检索（retrieval）机制的显著影响。

Method: 以50个问题、6个引擎、每引擎15次运行构建300个问题-引擎单元格，对1470个裁决组织进行开放抽取；对比5个无搜索引擎与1个检索启用引擎；另设4个深层单元格（运行至24次）与并行固定名册抽取作为对照；使用精确稀疏化与Chao2丰富度估计进行定量分析。

Result: 无搜索引擎在15次运行时86-92%的单元格仍新增从未见过的品牌（中位数名录15-31个组织）；检索启用引擎列表更短（中位数8个组织，64%的单元格仍在新增）。4个深层单元格运行至24次时仍持续新增域名，Chao2下界估计仅观测到59-84%；44%的检索引擎广度单元格在第15次运行仍在新增域名。单次运行仅展示五次运行品牌集的62-77%；各引擎中位问题抽取38个组织，其中15个仅出现在单一引擎。固定名册抽取在同响应下呈现平坦曲线，表明名册受限追踪会人为制造平台期。

Conclusion: 开放抽取（open extraction）揭示语言模型品牌推荐在重复提问下存在持续新颖性，引擎间重合度有限；启用检索会加速饱和但未必完全收敛；固定名册方法会人为制造"平台期"假象，方法论选择对结论影响显著。

Abstract: Whether repeated identical buying questions exhaust a language model's brand recommendations depends on retrieval. Across 300 question-engine cells (50 questions, six engines, 15 runs each, open extraction over 1,470 adjudicated organizations), the five engines answering without web search were still adding never-seen brands at run 15 in 86-92% of cells, with median repertoires of 15-31 organizations; the one retrieval-enabled engine closed its list (median 8 organizations, 64% of cells still adding), matching four earlier deep cells where web-search runs saturated by run ten. Cited-domain accumulation keeps rising at every horizon tested: four deep cells were still adding domains at run 24 with 59-84% of the Chao2 lower-bound estimate observed, and 44% of the retrieval engine's breadth cells were still adding domains at run 15. A single run shows 62-77% of the five-run brand set, and across engines the median question draws 38 organizations, of which a median of 15 appear in exactly one engine. Estimators are exact rarefaction and Chao2 richness; a parallel fixed-roster extraction reproduces flat curves on identical responses, so roster-bounded tracking manufactures plateaus that open extraction removes.

</details>


### [97] [Beyond Co-purchase Relation: Evolution of Complementary Recommendations at Allegro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05063)
*Aleksandra Osowska-Kurczab,Klaudia Nazarko,Eliška Kosturová,Lidia Wojciechowska,Michał Bień*

Main category: cs.IR
