TL;DR: 本文提出了AlleCompanion，一个在Allegro.com电商平台部署的生产级互补商品推荐检索框架。该框架通过数据级过滤启发式和类别约束的双塔架构，将大规模共同购买行为中的噪声信号转化为精确的语义兼容性。同时引入ComCat多源互补类别映射层，整合专家规则、人工反馈、LLM推理和统计挖掘。实验表明该方法有效过滤共同购买噪声，服务2000万月活用户，显著提升了自然发现和赞助投放的GMV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准互补商品推荐模型无法区分"仅仅是经常一起购买的商品"和"真正功能互补的商品"。例如，购买专业相机时，系统应推荐匹配的镜头而非通用三脚架。大规模共同购买流量中存在固有噪声，使得建模真实的用户行为变得困难。

Method: 1) 结合数据级过滤启发式与类别约束的双塔架构（Two Tower），其中Category Adapter在嵌入空间中引导模型，将候选商品限制在逻辑互补的类别边界内。2) 提出ComCat（多源互补类别映射），作为翻译层从噪声流量中提取有意义模式，整合专家规则、人在环反馈、LLM推理和统计挖掘。

Result: 实验表明，将显式类别约束与神经架构相结合可有效过滤共同购买噪声，推荐结果满足真实用户需求。该框架部署于Allegro.com，每月服务超过2000万活跃用户，在自然发现渠道带来显著的GMV提升，并在赞助投放中推动大幅收入增长。

Conclusion: 将显式的类别级约束与神经架构相结合，能够有效区分功能互补商品与单纯共同购买商品，从而在生产级电商平台上生成更精准的互补商品推荐，同时带来可观的商业收益。

Abstract: When a customer adds a professional camera to their cart, should the system suggest a matching lens, a generic tripod, or another camera body? Complementary Product Recommendation is vital for comprehensive basket building, yet standard models often fail to distinguish between items that are merely bought together and those that truly work together. In this paper, we present AlleCompanion: a production-scale retrieval framework deployed at Allegro.com that transforms noisy behavioural signals into precise semantic compatibility. We mitigate the intrinsic noise in large-scale co-purchase traffic by combining data-level filtering heuristics with a category-constrained Two Tower architecture. Within this framework, the Category Adapter guides the model in the embedding space, constraining candidates within logically complementary boundaries. Since modelling authentic user behaviour at scale is inherently difficult, we introduce ComCat, a multi-source Complementary Categories Mapping. ComCat acts as a translational layer that distils meaningful patterns from noisy traffic into a maintainable and controllable solution, integrating expert rules, human-in-the-loop feedback, LLM-based reasoning, and statistical mining. Our experimental results demonstrate that combining explicit category-level constraints with neural architectures effectively filters out co-purchase noise to surface recommendations that satisfy real-world user needs. Serving over 20 million active users monthly, the framework delivers significant uplifts in attributed GMV for organic discovery and drives substantial revenue growth in sponsored placements.

</details>


### [98] [Embedding Surgery: Localized Updates for Adaptive Ranking Correction in Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05110)
*Maddalena Amendola,Antonio Mallia,Raffaele Perego*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"embedding surgery"（嵌入手术）方法，一种轻量级的稠密检索自适应排序修正技术，通过在查询时对选定的文档嵌入进行局部最小化更新，遵循排序约束，从而实现在线反馈下的动态排名修正。在多个TREC基准和MS MARCO上取得显著提升（nDCG@10最高提升+60.64%），且无需重建索引，与现有查询适应方法互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索系统是现代搜索引擎和检索增强生成的核心组件，但由于文档嵌入是离线计算并存储在静态索引中的，这些系统难以适应用户反馈或不断演变的搜索意图。现有方法缺乏一种在保持全局嵌入结构的同时，利用反馈信号进行轻量级在线排序修正的手段。

Method: 将排序修正建模为一个凸优化问题：在查询时对选定的文档嵌入施加局部最小化更新，以编辑反馈、用户交互或大语言模型伪标签为导向，通过施加排序约束来最小化对受影响文档表示的修改量。该方法可无缝集成到标准稠密检索管线中，对ANN索引采用就地覆写方式实现。

Result: 在TREC Deep Learning、TREC Robust、TREC CAsT和MS MARCO基准上均取得一致性改进，在DL-Hard上采用编辑反馈时nDCG@10相对提升高达+60.64%。方法在噪声或漂移反馈下依然稳健，计算成本低，且不破坏嵌入空间的全局结构。排序修正能传播到语义相关的查询，并且无需重建索引即可安全高效地更新ANN索引。与Rocchio等查询适应方法互补，能产生额外增益且对噪声反馈更鲁棒。

Conclusion: Embedding surgery为稠密检索系统提供了一种实用且高效的自适应排序修正方案，克服了静态文档索引难以适应反馈的局限。该方法在性能、鲁棒性和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，可作为现有查询端适应方法的有效补充，为稠密检索系统的动态化演进提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems are core components of modern search engines, recommendation platforms, and retrieval-augmented generation pipelines. They encode documents and queries into dense embeddings, enabling efficient semantic search via vector similarity. However, because document embeddings are computed offline and stored in static indexes, these systems struggle to adapt to user feedback or evolving search intent. To address this limitation, we introduce \emph{embedding surgery}, a lightweight approach for adaptive ranking correction in dense retrieval. The method applies localized, minimal updates to selected document embeddings at query time, guided by editorial feedback, user interactions, or pseudo-labels from large language models. We formulate embedding surgery as a convex optimization problem that enforces ranking constraints while minimizing modifications to the affected document representations. We integrate embedding surgery into standard dense retrieval pipelines and evaluate it on TREC Deep Learning, TREC Robust, TREC CAsT, and MS MARCO benchmarks. Results show consistent improvements (e.g., up to +60.64\% relative improvement in nDCG@10 on DL-Hard under editorial feedback), even under noisy or shifting feedback, with low computational cost and without disrupting the global structure of the embedding space. Extensive experiments show that ranking corrections propagate to semantically related queries and that embedding updates can be applied safely and efficiently to scalable Approximate Nearest Neighbor indexes via simple in-place overwriting, without requiring costly index reconstruction. Finally, embedding surgery complements query adaptation methods such as CoRocchio, yielding additional gains while being more robust to noisy feedback.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Performance Study of Serverless Workloads in Confidential Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04478)
*Rikesh Niroula,Jianchen Shan,Xiaoning Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对机密虚拟机（CVM）中的无服务器工作负载进行了实证研究，系统分析了内存效率和冷/热启动的运行时开销。研究发现CVM因加密内存禁用跨VM页去重并降低内存可回收性，导致显著内存开销；热启动中频繁VMEXIT的工作负载（特别是空闲转换）受到严重性能下降，且vCPU与内存耦合配置会放大此问题；冷启动方面，在同一CVM内整合多个同函数容器并选择性放宽隔离机制可大幅降低启动开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密无服务器计算正成为医疗、金融和机器学习等需要强保密性保障的应用领域的关键范式。尽管CVM通过在不可信云环境中加密客户机内存来保护无服务器工作负载，但CVM对无服务器系统的影响尚未被充分理解，亟需系统性研究。

Method: 对CVM中的无服务器工作负载进行实证研究，系统覆盖内存效率以及在热启动和冷启动场景下的运行时开销分析。冷启动研究聚焦于容器创建，并探索在同一CVM中整合同一函数的多个容器以提升效率，测试选择性放宽隔离机制对启动开销的影响。

Result: CVM产生大量内存开销（加密内存禁用跨VM页去重并降低内存可回收性），限制了固定内存预算下的热容器容量；热启动运行时开销取决于工作负载，频繁VMEXIT（尤其空闲转换）导致显著性能下降，且被vCPU-内存耦合配置放大；冷启动方面，容器整合加选择性放宽隔离机制可大幅减少启动开销。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了机密无服务器计算的主要性能权衡，并提出了提升效率和降低延迟的实际方法。

Abstract: Confidential serverless computing is rapidly emerg- ing as a critical paradigm for application domains requiring strong confidentiality guarantees, such as healthcare, finance, and machine learning. To enable this paradigm in untrusted cloud environments, Confidential Virtual Machines (CVMs) provide isolation by encrypting the entire guest memory, and thus securing serverless workloads against host-level access and inter- ference. However, the implications of CVMs for serverless systems remain insufficiently understood. This paper presents an empirical study of serverless work- loads in CVMs, systematically covering memory efficiency and runtime overhead on both warm-starts and cold-starts. Our results show that CVMs incur substantial memory overhead because encrypted memory disables cross-VM page deduplication and reduces memory reclaimability, thereby limiting warm- container capacity under a fixed memory budget. Runtime overhead in warm-starts is workload dependent. Workloads with frequent VMEXITs, particularly idle transitions, suffer substantial slowdowns. These slowdowns are further amplified by common serverless deployment practice that couples vCPU allocation to memory size, as higher-memory configurations expose more vCPUs than some functions can use effectively. For cold starts, the study focuses on container creation, a common and major contributor to startup latency. Motivated by the memory-efficiency results, we consider the deployments in which multiple containers of the same function are consolidated within the same CVM to improve efficiency, and show that selectively relaxing certain isolation mechanisms in this setting can substan- tially reduce startup overhead. These results clarify the main performance tradeoffs of confidential serverless computing and suggest practical ways to improve efficiency and latency.

</details>


### [100] [Atlas: Optimizing Deployment of Compound AI Workflows on Heterogeneous Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04513)
*Milos Gravara,Andrija Stanisic,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Atlas框架，用于在SLO约束下优化复合AI工作流的部署。Atlas利用Markovian Accuracy Predictor (MAP)预测配置精度，并将执行计划选择建模为混合整数线性规划问题，在四个复合AI工作流上实现最高0.947的Spearman相关性，同时将部署成本降低多达42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合AI工作流在异质集群上的部署面临巨大的配置选择空间，每个阶段可能使用不同的AI模型和资源需求。系统指标可以通过逐阶段分析和拓扑组合获得，但精度无法如此预测，因为上游阶段的误差和信息损失会传播并影响下游阶段。现有方法要么端到端全面分析完整配置（扩展性差），要么使用假设阶段独立的乘积式精度代理（可能错误排序候选方案），缺乏高效又准确的精度估计方法。

Method: Atlas框架包含两个核心组件：(1) MAP (Markovian Accuracy Predictor)，一种基于马尔可夫假设的精度预测器，将中间输出离散化为精度桶，并通过相邻工作流阶段之间的局部条件精度转移来组合转移档案，从而无需穷举端到端分析即可估计配置精度；(2) 将执行计划选择形式化为混合整数线性规划(MILP)，在满足SLO约束的条件下最大化预测精度。

Result: 跨四个复合AI工作流，MAP实现了最高0.947的Spearman相关性，同时相对于穷举端到端分析，分析成本降低了最多2.6倍。在Atlas优化器引导下，选择的执行计划在oracle精度的0.03以内，并通过异质化部署将部署成本降低了多达42%。

Conclusion: Atlas通过利用相邻阶段之间的马尔可夫性质（条件精度转移），有效地解决了复合AI工作流部署中精度预测的扩展性问题，在保证预测精度的同时大幅降低了分析成本，并通过MILP优化实现了SLO约束下的精度-成本权衡。

Abstract: Compound AI workflows are increasingly used to serve complex AI tasks by coordinating multiple AI models and software components. This approach enables deployment flexibility, as each workflow stage can expose different model variants and resource requirements, but it also expands the deployment choices. A deployment must choose an execution plan that selects AI models for each compound AI workflow stage and places them on a heterogeneous cluster in order to satisfy SLOs. Deployment optimizers therefore need estimates to compare many candidate plans and identify feasible ones. System metrics can often be profiled per stage and composed according to workflow topology, but accuracy cannot, as errors and information loss at upstream stages affect the accuracy of downstream stages. Existing approaches either profile complete configurations end to end, which scales poorly, or use product-based accuracy surrogates that treat stages as independent and can misrank candidate plans. We introduce Atlas, a framework for optimizing compound AI deployments under SLO constraints. Atlas uses MAP, a Markovian Accuracy Predictor, to estimate configuration accuracy from local conditional accuracy transitions between adjacent workflow stages. MAP discretizes intermediate outputs into accuracy buckets and composes transition profiles according to workflow topology, giving the optimizer an accuracy estimate without exhaustive end-to-end profiling. Atlas formulates execution-plan selection as a mixed-integer linear program that maximizes predicted accuracy subject to SLOs. Across four compound AI workflows, MAP achieves Spearman correlation up to 0.947 while reducing profiling cost by up to 2.6x relative to exhaustive end-to-end profiling. Guided by MAP, the Atlas optimizer selects execution plans within 0.03 of oracle accuracy while reducing deployment cost by up to 42% through heterogeneous placement.

</details>


### [101] [Revisiting MemGuard Overhead: A Reproduction Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04547)
*Weifan Chen,Heechul Yun,Renato Mancuso*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文报告指出，先前关于MemGuard内存带宽调节器开销的研究因使用了旧版本且配置不当（将基准测试固定在与处理全局定时器中断的主核上），导致其1.79x的开销被无意放大。通过在现代去中心化实现上复现实验，真实开销显著下降，与MemPol相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MemPol先前研究声称MemGuard存在高达1.79x的显著开销，但这一对比可能因实验配置和版本问题而失真。本文旨在澄清这些实验结果，为内存带宽调节器的公平比较提供准确的基线指标。

Method: 重现MemPol实验环境，分析其使用的MemGuard旧版本及配置缺陷（基准测试被固定在与全局定时器中断处理相关的主核上），并使用现代去中心化实现的MemGuard在相同条件下重新执行实验进行对比。

Result: 使用配置正确且较新的MemGuard实现后，其实际执行开销在相同条件下显著下降，且与MemPol的开销高度可比。

Conclusion: 先前工作中MemGuard的高开销归因于配置不当和旧版本限制，而非机制本身的固有缺陷。修正后的结果表明MemGuard与MemPol的开销相当，为未来内存带宽调节器的公平评估提供了更新且全面的视角。

Abstract: As an increasing number of embedded platforms incorporate multiple processing units, shared resource contention induced unpredictable execution time poses a challenge for real-time system design. Memory bandwidth regulation is a popular mitigation approach, and MemGuard is the canonical example. Recently, MemPol introduced a new bandwidth regulation mechanism, which was compared with MemGuard. Specifically, they reported significant overheads for MemGuard, citing up to a 1.79x slowdown, to contextualize MemPol's comparative benefits.
  This report is meant to clarify and add the necessary nuance to the experiments carried out in that prior work. Specifically, we show that the MemGuard overheads presented in these prior evaluations were unintentionally amplified as the result of using a suboptimal configuration with an older version of MemGuard, wherein the benchmark under test was pinned directly to the master core responsible for handling global timer interrupts. By faithfully reproducing these specific experiments using a modern, decentralized implementation of MemGuard, we demonstrate that the actual execution overhead drops significantly under identical conditions.
  Consequently, when evaluated with a properly configured recent version, MemGuard exhibits an overhead that is highly comparable to MemPol's overhead. By revisiting these baseline metrics, this report provides an updated and comprehensive perspective required for future evaluations of memory bandwidth regulators.

</details>


### [102] [Improving Progressive Compression with Adaptive Interpolation and Coefficient Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04573)
*Wenbo Li,Xuan Wu,Qian Gong,Pu Jiao,Jieyang Chen,Qing Liu,Norbert Podhorszki,Scott Klasky,Xin Liang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应渐进压缩框架，通过两种互补插值方案和系数分解方法，针对误差界和峰值信噪比两种常用目标提升科学数据的渐进检索效率，在压缩比和端到端数据传输性能上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百亿亿次（Exascale）模拟产生的数据远超存储和分析能力，需要高效的数据缩减。现有有损压缩需在压缩时固定误差容限，而渐进压缩虽放松了此限制，但仍依赖固定的重构策略，未能充分利用分解后系数间的相关性，限制了渐进检索效率。

Method: 本文提出三方面创新：(1) 利用两种互补插值方案进行自适应渐进压缩，并针对不同目标优化以提升效率；(2) 提出系数分解方法，利用去相关后数据中常被忽视的空间相关性；(3) 开发自适应渐进压缩工作流，自动选择最佳匹配的重构流水线并进行定制优化。

Result: 在五个真实科学数据集上与三种最先进的渐进压缩器对比，在相同误差容限下压缩比最高提升42.3%，在相同PSNR下压缩比最高提升92.5%。传输512GB科学数据到远程站点时，端到端数据传输性能最高提升1.26倍；在检索最少数据量的同时实现了最高的可视化质量。

Conclusion: 该框架通过自适应选择重构策略和利用系数空间相关性，显著提升了渐进压缩在误差界和PSNR两种目标下的检索效率，兼具高压缩比、高数据传输性能和高质量可视化效果，为大规模科学数据压缩提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Exascale simulations generate data far faster than it can be stored or analyzed, making efficient data reduction essential. Error-controlled lossy compression offers high compression ratios under user-specified error bounds, but the target tolerance must be fixed at compression time. Progressive compression relaxes this restriction, yet existing methods still rely on fixed refactoring strategies and do not fully exploit correlations among decomposed coefficients, limiting the efficiency of progressive retrieval. In this work, we present an adaptive progressive compression framework that improves retrieval efficiency for two common targets, namely error-bound and peak Signal-to-Noise ratios. Our contributions are fourfold. (1) We propose to leverage two complementary interpolation schemes for adaptive progressive compression toward different targets, and we optimize them to achieve high efficiency. (2) We propose coefficient decomposition, a novel method that exploits the commonly overlooked spatial correlations among decorrelated data, which further improves the efficiency. (3) We develop the adaptive progressive compression workflow with automatic selection of the best-fit refactoring pipeline and tailored optimizations. (4) We evaluate the proposed framework on five real-world scientific datasets against three state-of-the-art progressive compressors. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework improves the compression ratio by up to $42.3\%$ under the same requested error tolerance and up to $92.5\%$ at the same PSNR, compared with the best-performing existing methods. When transferring $512$ GB of scientific data to remote sites, the framework delivers up to $1.26\times$ speedup in the end-to-end data transfer performance. Furthermore, our method achieves the highest visualization quality while retrieving the least amount of data from storage.

</details>


### [103] [CIERA: Cross-Iteration Exponent Reuse for Lossless Allgather in Sharded MoE Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04609)
*Ali Zafar Sadiq,Haiying Shen,Masahiro Tanaka*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CIERA，一种针对MoE模型分片数据并行训练的无损通信压缩方法。利用跨迭代指数稳定性，在接收端缓存权重指数，仅传输符号和尾数，从而实现精确重构并显著减少Allgather通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MoE模型训练中，分片数据并行需要Allgather操作来重构完整的权重矩阵，这一通信操作常常主导迭代时间。现有有损压缩方法牺牲数值精度，而现有无损方法未能利用跨迭代的指数稳定性，因此需要一种既能无损又能有效降低通信开销的方法。

Method: CIERA方法的关键在于观察经过短暂预热后，大多数权重的指数值在多次迭代中保持不变。其策略是：在本地缓存指数，当指数未变化时仅传输符号和尾数；接收端将缓存的指数与接收的数据结合，精确重构原始权重。此外，按层判断是否应用压缩（仅在能节省时间时使用），并将压缩操作与Allgather通信和计算重叠执行。

Result: 在16块GPU上运行OLMoE-1B-7B模型时，CIERA相比无损基线和有损基线分别实现3.70倍和3.68倍加速；在128块GPU场景下预计可实现4.28倍和4.42倍加速。所有评估运行均保持逐位精确的参数重构。

Conclusion: CIERA通过利用跨迭代的指数稳定性，实现了系统感知的无损通信压缩方法，在保持参数逐位精确重构的同时显著提升MoE训练效率，且加速效果随GPU数量扩展而增强。

Abstract: In training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models, sharded data parallelism partitions each expert's parameters across GPUs, requiring an Allgather operation to reconstruct the full weight matrix before each layer executes. This communication often dominates iteration time. Prior work often reduces this overhead using lossy compression methods that sacrifice numerical fidelity, while existing lossless methods do not exploit cross-iteration exponent stability. In this paper, we propose Cross-Iteration Exponent Reuse Allgather (CIERA), a lossless, system-aware communication method for sharded MoE training. We observe that after a brief warmup phase, the exponent values of most weights remain unchanged across iterations. Based on this, we cache exponents locally and transmit only the sign and mantissa when exponents are unchanged. The receiver reconstructs the original weights exactly by combining the cached exponents with the received data. Since parameter matrices vary in shape across layers, compression is applied only when it yields net time savings. Moreover, the compression operation is overlapped with both Allgather communication and computation. Our real experiments and large-scale trace-driven simulator show that on OLMoE-1B-7B at 16 GPUs, CIERA achieves a 3.70x speedup over the lossless baseline and 3.68x over the lossy baseline, projected to reach 4.28x and 4.42x respectively at 128 GPUs, while preserving bitwise-exact parameter reconstruction in all evaluated runs.

</details>


### [104] [GreenPipe: Power Modeling for Containerized DNN Inference on Kubernetes Edge Nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04952)
*Mengxue Wang,Peini Liu,Amir Taherkordi,Jordi Guitart*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GreenPipe提出了一种面向容器化边缘-云环境中分布式DNN推理的自动化多资源功率估算流水线，在无RAPL等硬件功率计数器的ARM节点上，通过外部功率计测量构建多资源回归模型，并按比例将功率归因到各容器。在树莓派4上实现系统级MAPE为6.3-9.4%，比基线平均提升26.9%的MAPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限的ARM边缘节点缺乏RAPL等硬件功率计数器，且仅基于CPU的模型无法捕捉多资源（CPU、内存、网络等）行为，导致分布式DNN推理场景下的在线功率估算不准确。

Method: GreenPipe包含自动化分析-训练-验证流水线：从外部功率计获取测量数据，构建多资源回归模型，并将总功率按比例分配给各个容器；在K3s边缘云测试床的树莓派4上进行评估。

Result: 系统级MAPE为6.3-9.4%，较CPU压力和仅利用率基线平均改善26.9%的MAPE；同时报告推理延迟和每次推理能耗，揭示了不同工作负载配置下的性能-能耗权衡。

Conclusion: GreenPipe在无硬件功率计数器的ARM边缘节点上实现了准确的多资源功率估算与容器级功率归因，并能联合报告延迟与能耗，为容器化边缘场景的性能-能源优化提供了有效支撑。

Abstract: Distributed DNN inference is increasingly deployed in containerized edge-cloud environments, where workloads run on-device or are exposed to remote clients over the network. Accurate online power estimation on resource-constrained ARM nodes without hardware power counters such as RAPL remains a challenge, and CPU-only models fail to capture multi-resource behavior. We present GreenPipe, an automated profiling-training-validation pipeline that builds multi-resource regression models from external power meter measurements and attributes power to containers proportionally. GreenPipe is evaluated on a Raspberry Pi 4 edge node in a K3s edge-cloud testbed, covering DNN inference with three vision models, multiple precisions, thread counts, and both local and serving scenarios. System-level MAPE is 6.3-9.4%, improving over CPU-stress and utilization-only baselines by 26.9% MAPE on average. We jointly report inference latency and energy per inference, exposing performance-energy trade-offs across workload configurations.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [DTM: Deterministic Approaches for Black-box Test Suite Minimization with Tree-based Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04205)
*Md Siam,Shartaz Sajid Nahid,Md Arif Hasan,Nurul Ahad Tawhid,Kazi Sakib*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DTM框架，用于黑盒测试套件最小化，通过AST相似度计算和三种确定性算法（改进的最小生成树、谱聚类、动态规划）实现确定性测试套件缩减，在Defects4J的16个Java项目661个缺陷版本上验证，平均准确率0.74，执行时间0.98分钟，且多次运行结果完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效的黑盒测试套件最小化方法依赖进化搜索算法，引入非确定性，导致多次运行结果不一致，削弱了自动化测试管道的可靠性。

Method: DTM框架将测试用例转换为抽象语法树（AST），使用四种基于树的度量计算两两相似度；子集选择采用三种确定性算法：改进的最小生成树、谱聚类和动态规划。

Result: 在Defects4J中16个Java项目661个缺陷版本上，DTM平均准确率达0.74，执行时间仅0.98分钟，优于所有现有先进方法，且多次运行结果完全一致，确保完全确定性。

Conclusion: DTM在保持测试套件最小化效果和效率的同时，实现了完全确定性，解决了现有进化搜索方法因非确定性导致的结果不稳定问题，为自动化测试流水线提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Black-box Test Suite Minimization (TSM) techniques reduce testing costs without requiring access to production code. However, existing effective approaches rely on evolutionary search algorithms, introducing non-determinism that produces inconsistent results across runs, undermining reliability in automated testing pipelines. We propose DTM (Deterministic approaches for black-box Test suite Minimization), a framework that ensures deterministic test suite reduction while preserving effectiveness and efficiency. DTM converts test cases into Abstract Syntax Trees and computes pairwise similarities using four tree-based measures. For subset selection, it employs three deterministic algorithms: Modified Minimum Spanning Tree, Spectral Clustering, and Dynamic Programming. We evaluated DTM on 16 Java projects from Defects4J with 661 buggy versions. Experimental results show that DTM achieved an average accuracy of 0.74 with an execution time of just 0.98 minutes, outperforming all state-of-the-art approaches. Moreover, it consistently produced identical results across multiple runs, ensuring full determinism.

</details>


### [106] [Breaking the Alphabet: Rethinking File Ordering in Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04207)
*Md Shamimur Rahman,Zadia Codabux,Chanchal K. Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这是首个针对1,355名专业开发者的大规模调查研究，探讨Pull Request中文件排序如何影响代码审查效果。研究发现默认的字母排序与人类认知不匹配，57.6%的开发者认为它增加了上下文切换和审阅疲劳，63.9%担忧会导致漏掉bug，66%的开发者希望获得依赖感知分组和可自定义排序功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码审查对软件质量至关重要，但关于PR中文件排序如何影响审查效果的研究非常有限。主流工具默认按字母排序，优先考虑可预测性而非上下文相关性，这种排序方式如何影响认知负荷和审查全面性尚不清楚。

Method: 采用混合方法，对182个广泛使用的开源项目中的1,355名专业开发者进行大规模问卷调查，分析文件排序对审查行为、理解和感知效果的影响。

Result: 仅10.2%的审查者认为字母排序最优；57.6%报告它增加上下文切换、干扰逻辑推理并导致审查疲劳；63.9%担忧默认排序可能遗漏bug；66%要求依赖感知分组和可自定义文件排序。

Conclusion: 文件排序的默认设计存在认知错位，需要以审查者为中心的界面设计，使工具行为更好地与人类认知对齐，包括依赖感知分组和可自定义排序等功能改进。

Abstract: Effective code review is central to maintaining software quality, yet there is limited research about how the ordering of changed files in Pull Requests (PRs) influences review effectiveness. Most popular code review tools default to alphabetical ordering, favoring predictability over contextual relevance. While prior studies examined how file position shapes reviewer attention, it remains unclear how such ordering influences cognitive load and perceived review thoroughness. This study presents the first large-scale survey of 1,355 professional developers across 182 widely used open-source projects to investigate how file ordering impacts review behavior, comprehension, and perceived effectiveness. Our mixed-methods analysis reveals that only 10.2% of reviewers consider alphabetical ordering optimal, underscoring a cognitive misalignment in their interpretation of code changes. Although some developers appreciate its predictability, more than half (57.6%) report that it increases context switching, disrupts logical reasoning, and contributes to review fatigue, and 63.9% expressed concern that the default ordering may cause them to miss bugs. We further identify key challenges in multi-file reviews and elicit developers' expectations for improved tooling, including dependency-aware grouping and customizable file ordering (requested by 66% of reviewers). These findings highlight the need for reviewer-centric interface designs that better align tool behavior with human cognition.

</details>


### [107] [AI Writes Code, Humans Pay the Debt. An Empirical Study on the Sustainability and Evolution of Agent-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04208)
*Antonino Coppola,Matteo Esposito,Rick Kazman,Valentina Lenarduzzi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项大规模仓库挖掘研究，旨在通过SQuaD数据集中的62.8万个issue票证，系统比较GenAI编码代理生成的代码与开发者实际提交在问题定位准确性、技术债务引入及其长期演化方面的差异，以揭示代理驱动开发对软件质量的真实影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI编码代理在软件工程中的采用日益广泛，短期生产率收益明显，但其对软件质量和长期技术债务的影响尚不明确，缺乏大规模实证证据。

Method: 采用大规模仓库挖掘方法：基于SQuaD数据集构建62.8万issue票证候选集；为这些问题生成基于代理的实现；使用静态分析指标和工具与开发者的实际提交进行对比；在提交级别和版本发布级别分别分析差异；通过系统化基准测试策略选取多个LLM代理。

Result: 预期结果包括：代理开发与传统开发在定位准确性、技术债务引入方面的差异证据；跨LLM模型间的差异表现；长期演化中潜在的分歧模式。

Conclusion: 研究成果将丰富对含代理软件演化的理解，为实践中更负责任地采用编码代理提供实证依据和决策参考。

Abstract: Context. The increasing adoption of Generative AI coding agents in software engineering is transforming how developers implement and maintain code. While these systems provide short-term productivity benefits, their long-term impact on software quality and technical debt remains unclear. Aim. We aim to investigate how agent-generated code affects software quality, focusing on issue localization accuracy, the introduction of technical debt, and its evolution over time. Method. We will conduct a large-scale mining software repositories study using the SQuaD dataset, employing a candidate set of 628k issue tickets. We will generate agent-based implementations for these issues, and compare them with the actual commits done by developers using static analysis metrics and tools. We will analyze differences at the commit level and across releases, and we will consider multiple LLM-based Agents selected through a systematic benchmarking strategy. Expected Results. We expect to provide empirical evidence on the trade-offs introduced by agent-based development, including differences in localization accuracy, variations in technical debt introduction, and potential divergence in long-term evolution. We expect the results to highlight variability across LLMs, to enrich our understanding of software evolution with Agents, and to inform more responsible adoption of Agents in software development.

</details>


### [108] [The Prompt Triangle: A Registered Report on Prompts as Hybrid Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04209)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Jan-Philipp Steghöfer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究是一项注册报告，提出Stage 1协议，通过受控实验（n=30）、社区上传数据和挖掘数据，验证四个关于提示词（prompt）演变、开发者特征、需求工程活动对齐及时序模式的假设，探究AI辅助编程中提示词如何作为软件工程制品演变，及其与需求工程对齐程度是否能预测开发成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程助手正将软件开发的重心从编写代码转向编写提示词，但关于提示词如何作为软件工程制品发挥作用，目前仍缺乏实证证据。

Method: 采用注册报告形式，预注册四个假设，使用受控实验（n=30）、社区上传数据和挖掘数据，并通过双编码方法进行测试，基于Prompt Triangle概念模型分析提示词的三个组成部分（功能与质量、通用解决方案、特定解决方案）。

Result: 论文为Stage 1注册报告，呈现研究协议，尚无具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在确认提示词演变规律及其与需求工程活动的对齐程度是否能够预测开发成果，从而为AI辅助编程中的提示词工程提供实证基础。

Abstract: AI-based coding assistants are transforming software development by shifting effort from writing code to crafting prompts that guide code generation. Despite their growing importance, little empirical evidence exists on how prompts function as software engineering artifacts. Building on prior work framing prompts as mixed artifacts combining requirement intent and solution guidance, we build on the Prompt Triangle, a conceptual model describing prompts along three components: Functionality and Quality (requirements), General Solutions (architectural guidance), and Specific Solutions (implementation constraints). This registered report presents the Stage 1 protocol for a confirmatory study investigating how prompts evolve and how their alignment with requirements engineering (RE) activities predicts development outcomes. We preregister four hypotheses examining prompt evolution, developer characteristics, RE activity alignment, and temporal patterns. We employ a controlled experiment (n=30), community uploads, and mined data using dual-coding to test whether prompting patterns predict development success.

</details>


### [109] [SH-PDOPS: AI-Driven Cloud Native Enterprise Reliability Framework for Predictive Analytics and Intelligent DevOps Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04210)
*Ayushman Bosu Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI驱动的云原生企业可靠性框架，通过整合机器学习模型、Kubernetes编排、可观测性管道和自动化事件响应机制，实现预测性故障检测、异常监控、自愈基础设施和CI/CD优化，实验证明该框架能够提升运维效率、减少停机时间并增强企业环境的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式基础设施面临日益复杂的可靠性挑战，传统DevOps实践难以应对动态云原生环境中的故障预测、异常发现和自动化响应需求，亟需将人工智能与可靠性工程相结合以提升系统韧性。

Method: 提出一个整合机器学习模型、Kubernetes编排、可观测性管道和自动化事件响应机制的AI驱动云原生企业可靠性框架，涵盖预测性故障检测、异常监控、自愈基础设施和CI/CD优化等核心能力。

Result: 实验评估表明该框架在运维效率、系统停机时间和可扩展性方面均有显著改善，能够满足企业级环境的可靠性需求。

Conclusion: 该框架为实现AI与DevOps方法论的融合提供了实用路径，能够帮助企业实现自适应和自主化的基础设施管理。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven cloud-native enterprise reliability framework designed to improve predictive analytics, intelligent DevOps automation, and system resilience in modern distributed infrastructures. The proposed framework integrates machine learning models, Kubernetes orchestration, observability pipelines, and automated incident response mechanisms to enhance reliability engineering practices. The study explores predictive failure detection, anomaly monitoring, self-healing infrastructure, and CI/CD optimization using cloud-native technologies. Experimental evaluation demonstrates improved operational efficiency, reduced downtime, and enhanced scalability for enterprise environments. The framework provides a practical approach for combining artificial intelligence with DevOps methodologies to achieve adaptive and autonomous infrastructure management.

</details>


### [110] [Data-Related Challenges and Requirements for Event Log Generation in Process Mining: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04211)
*Ghita El Alaoui Talibi,Oleksandr Kosenkov,Anastasija Nikiforova*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对流程挖掘中事件日志生成阶段的数据质量问题进行了系统性文献综述，识别出29个数据相关挑战和5类基本需求类别，发现现有解决方案已嵌入数据工程和领域知识工程技术，旨在帮助研究者和实践者理解事件日志生成趋势，并利用数据工程与需求工程技术改进日志生成及流程挖掘结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流程挖掘已成为支持业务流程改进和未来数字经济软件技术的关键技术，其中数据质量是社区讨论中最紧迫的挑战。然而，事件日志生成作为流程挖掘的第一阶段，其与数据工程和需求工程的关系未得到足够关注。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，系统梳理事件日志生成中的数据相关挑战、需求和解决方案，并对文献进行分类和分析。

Result: 共识别出29个数据相关挑战，可用5类基本需求类别加以解决；现有解决方案已包含一定的数据工程和领域知识工程技术。

Conclusion: 该综述可为研究者和实践者理解事件日志生成当前趋势提供支撑，促进数据工程和需求工程技术的应用以改进日志生成，进而优化流程挖掘的整体效果。

Abstract: Process mining is becoming an essential technology that not only supports the improvement of business processes but also enables the software technologies underlying the future digital economy. Among the multiple challenges discussed by the process mining community, data quality is emerging as the most pressing one. However, insufficient attention has been paid to event log generation as the very first phase of process mining and to its relationship with data engineering and requirements engineering. To address this gap, we conducted a systematic literature review of data-related challenges, requirements, and solutions in event log generation. Our results show that a wide range of 29 challenges can be addressed with 5 basic categories of requirements. Existing solutions already embed some form of data engineering and domain knowledge engineering techniques for event log generation. This review can support researchers and practitioners in understanding current trends in event log generation for process mining and in leveraging data and requirements engineering techniques to improve log generation and, ultimately, process mining outcomes.

</details>


### [111] [Same Request, Different Answer: Quantization Amplifies Cache-Induced Divergence in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04748)
*Aditi Patodiya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了前缀缓存（prefix caching）对可复现性的影响。结果发现，启用缓存会改变供智能体轨迹：16位精度下36.2%的任务轨迹改变，4位量化下高达75%。禁用缓存时800个episode全部逐位一致，说明其他非确定性来源可忽略。缓存导致的不可复现根源在于缓存状态不在请求中且默认不被重置。作者发布了完整测试工具链和日志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流开源推理栈默认启用前缀缓存并将其视为透明优化，但缓存是否影响结果可复现性鲜有研究。作者希望量化这种"免费优化"在可复现性上的代价，尤其关注权重量化加剧下的影响。

Method: 在固定模型、解码参数、随机种子和请求顺序的条件下，以batch size=1串行执行80个episode的多轮智能体工具调用任务。跨越两个推理引擎和四种权重格式，分别启用和禁用缓存进行对比。通过三组实验定位偏差来源，并使用单轮桥接实验验证任务结果层面的影响。

Result: 启用缓存时，16位精度下36.2%的episode轨迹改变，4位精度下高达75.0%，且梯度在受控缓存配置下依然存在。禁用缓存的800个episode全部逐位一致。服务器级缓存设置可改变37.5个百分点的运行间偏差；恢复缓存状态后，缓存路径与重算路径各有40/40可复现但彼此仍有14项差异。

Conclusion: 缓存服务在给定缓存状态下是确定性的，但在实践中不可复现，因为缓存状态不在请求中且默认不重置。该问题随量化精度降低而显著加剧，并可影响任务结果而不改变总体准确率。

Abstract: Prefix caching, in which a serving engine reuses the key and value tensors of a shared prompt prefix across requests, is enabled by default in the major open-source stacks and treated as a transparent optimization. We measure what it costs in reproducibility, and find that the cost rises sharply with weight quantization. Holding the model, decoding parameters, seed, and request order fixed, and issuing every request serially at batch size one, we ran an eighty-episode multi-turn agentic tool-use workload with caching enabled and disabled across two engines and four weight formats. Enabling the cache changed the agent's trajectory on 36.2 percent of episodes at 16-bit precision and on 75.0 percent at four-bit, a gradient that survives re-measurement under a controlled cache configuration. With caching disabled, repeated execution was bit-identical in every configuration, 0 of 800 episodes, which bounds other sources of nondeterminism at 0.5 percent. Repeated cache-enabled runs did diverge, and three experiments locate the cause: a single server-level prompt-cache setting moves run-to-run divergence by 37.5 percentage points, execution order acts only while that setting is active, and restoring cache state makes the cached and recompute paths each reproduce on 40 of 40 items while still differing from each other on 14. Cached serving is deterministic given cache state, and irreproducible in practice because that state is absent from the request and never reset by default. A single-turn bridge shows the divergence reaching task outcomes without shifting aggregate accuracy. We release the harness, logs, and analysis pipeline.

</details>


### [112] [Big Questions on Software Architecture: Report of the ICSE 2026 BoF on Software Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04212)
*Davide Taibi,Patricia Lago,Henry Muccini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个社区倡议的初步结果，旨在识别和讨论软件架构（SA）研究中的重大科学问题，区分真正的科学挑战与技术趋势/炒作。内容来自2026年ICSE和ICSA会议的两个分会场，本文呈现第一场的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构的重要性日益凸显，但SA研究社区需要反思哪些是值得科学研究的大问题，哪些只是技术趋势或炒作。

Method: 通过社区倡议，在2026年ICSE和ICSA会议上组织两个分会场，收集和讨论SA研究中的重大科学问题。

Result: 本文报告第一分会场（session）的讨论结果，第二分会场的结果将在后续补充。

Conclusion: 该研究仍在进行中（"to be continued"），目前仅呈现了初步的社区讨论成果。

Abstract: Software Architecture (SA) is more important than ever, from the need to master the pervasive impact of technology in society and planet earth, to balancing the role of humans versus technology, flexibility versus chaos, economic convenience versus digital autonomy. Despite SA importance, we feel that the SA research community needs to reflect on what are the big questions that deserve scientific research, and what are instead technology trends or hypes. In this paper, we report the results of a community initiative aiming to identify and discuss the big questions in SA research. Organised in two sessions hosted in 2026 at the ICSE and ICSA conferences, respectively, this paper reports on the results collected during the first session, and looks forward to complementing them with the results of the second one. To be continued ...

</details>


### [113] [Toward Model-Driven Digital Twin Configuration: Separating Structure Semantics and Runtime with SysML SAREF and Ditto](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04213)
*Andrey Sadovykh,Matthew Rusakov,Kirill Korikov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张将能源社区数字孪生的配置任务拆分为三个互补工具协同完成：SysML建模语言负责系统结构、SAREF4ENER标准能源词汇负责语义含义、Eclipse Ditto（通过W3C WoT图描述）负责运行时表示。论文给出了从带语义标注的SysML模型推导Ditto配置的概念性规则（尚未实现为可执行生成器），并以Modelio建模的温室能源社区节点为例进行说明，论证该方法相比手工编写配置能提升可追溯性、配置一致性，并建立互操作所需的语义钩子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前能源社区（联合生产、存储和消费电力的邻里）日益依赖数字孪生来监控和控制太阳能板、电池、加热器等物理设备，但数字孪生的配置目前主要靠工程师手工编写：字段名随意、单位隐含、设备间关系缺乏机器可读的描述，导致其他系统无法可靠地解释配置。

Method: 提出将配置任务拆分为三个互补工具的分层方法：SysML建模能源社区的结构、SAREF4ENER提供标准化的能源领域语义词汇、Eclipse Ditto（基于W3C WoT Thing Description）承载运行时数字孪生表示；给出从SysML模型（以该词汇标注）推导Ditto配置的概念性规则。

Result: 以温室能源社区节点为案例，展示了从Modelio中建立的SysML模型推导出Ditto配置的可行路径；论证了该拆分方法相比手工编写配置，能提升可追溯性和配置一致性，并建立互操作所需的标准语义基础。

Conclusion: 该拆分方案在提升配置可追溯性和一致性方面具有前景，但目前仅提供概念性规则而非可执行生成器；贡献范围限定在设计期的配置阶段，存在一定局限，有待后续实现和验证。

Abstract: Energy communities, neighbourhoods that jointly produce, store, and consume electricity, increasingly rely on \emph{digital twins}: live software copies of physical devices such as a solar panel, a battery, or a heater, kept in sync for monitoring and control. Setting up such a twin is today mostly manual: an engineer hand-writes a configuration whose field names are arbitrary, whose units are implicit, and which carries no machine-readable description of how devices relate, so other systems cannot reliably interpret it. This position paper argues that the task should be split across three complementary tools, each doing what it does best: a modelling language for the system's structure (SysML), a standard energy vocabulary for its meaning (SAREF4ENER), and a digital-twin platform for the running representation (Eclipse Ditto, via the W3C Web-of-Things Thing Description). We give conceptual rules (not yet an executable generator) for deriving a Ditto configuration from a SysML model annotated with that vocabulary, illustrate them on a greenhouse energy-community node modelled in Modelio, and argue that the split improves traceability and configuration consistency, and establishes the semantic hooks needed for interoperability, over hand-written alternatives. We scope the contribution to design-time configuration and discuss its limits.

</details>


### [114] [A Mixed-Method Empirical Study of LLM Assistance in Software Engineering Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04214)
*Pamali D. Weerasinghe,Roshan N. Rajapakse,Isuru Dharmadasa,Chamath Keppitiyagama*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法实证研究大一与大四本科生在LLM辅助软件工程任务中的表现，发现LLM辅助的效果依赖于任务类型、开发者经验水平和验证实践，而非单纯的生成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛融入软件开发流程，但现有讨论往往未区分任务类型、开发者资历和验证需求，缺乏对这些变量如何影响LLM辅助效果的系统性理解。

Method: 采用混合方法：阶段1为初步问卷调查（n=157），表征两组学生的LLM使用暴露度、依赖度和信任校准；阶段2为基于任务的准实验（n=20），在AI辅助与非AI条件下比较实现、约束驱动算法选择和架构推理等结构化软件工程任务，并通过屏幕录制和行为定性编码分析绩效与行为轨迹。

Result: 调查显示LLM广泛采用且存在大量验证工作，不同年级在约束密集场景中对LLM能力的感知存在差异。准实验表明AI辅助改变了工作流结构，如AI优先任务进入、复制-转移集成和AI中介调试，而非AI工作流更多依赖文档、先前模板和迭代试错。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的收益并非由生成速度单一决定，而是受任务类型、专业经验和验证实践共同调节，需针对不同任务和开发者群体差异化评估AI辅助工具的价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into software development workflows, yet their effects are often discussed without distinguishing between task types, developer seniority, and verification demands. This paper presents a mixed-method empirical study of LLM-assisted software engineering with first-year and fourth-year undergraduates. Phase 1 is a preliminary survey (n=157) that characterizes LLM exposure, reliance, and trust calibration among the two groups. Phase 2 is a task-based quasi-experiment with a purposive sample from both cohorts (n=20). Here, we compare AI-assisted and non-AI conditions on a structured set of software engineering tasks spanning implementation, constraint-driven algorithm selection, and architectural reasoning. We then analyze performance outcomes alongside behavioral traces captured via screen recording and a qualitative coding process. Survey results indicate widespread LLM adoption and substantial verification effort, alongside cohort differences in perceived LLM capability for constraint-heavy scenarios. The quasi-experiment further shows that AI assistance changes workflow structure. For example, participants frequently adopt AI-first task entry, copy-transfer integration, and AI-mediated debugging, whereas non-AI workflows rely more on documentation, prior templates, and iterative trial-error refinement. Overall, our findings suggest that the benefits of LLM assistance are task-dependent and mediated by expertise and verification practices, rather than by generation speed alone.

</details>


### [115] [A systematic literature review on logging smell detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04215)
*Nora Madi,Manal Binkhonain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述（SLR）对21项有关日志气味检测的研究进行了梳理，分析了检测技术、数据集与评估方法，发现该领域研究仍分散且缺乏统一基准，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日志是软件开发中监控系统、理解行为和修复问题的重要手段，但不恰当的日志记录会产生日志气味缺陷，降低日志效用甚至引发问题。由于现有日志气味检测研究分散且不一致，需要系统梳理该领域的研究现状。

Method: 采用系统文献综述（SLR）方法，对21项聚焦日志气味检测的研究进行分析。研究定义了关键日志相关术语，将气味类型映射到现有分类体系，并审查了各研究中的检测技术、数据集和评估策略。

Result: 研究发现日志气味检测研究仍较为分散且不一致，缺乏通用的基准和标准化的评估方法导致研究之间难以比较；同时在不同研究中，所针对的气味类型和数量存在差异。

Conclusion: 日志气味的检测与研究仍有改进空间。作者指出了若干挑战，并建议未来方向包括开发更好的检测工具、利用大语言模型（LLMs）以及构建更标准化的评估数据集。

Abstract: Context:Logging is an important part of software development that helps developers monitor systems, understand behavior, and fix problems. But when logging is done poorly, it can introduce logging smells, which are defects that reduce the usefulness of logs or even make them problematic. Objective:This study looks at how logging smells are currently detected. The goal is to better understand the existing research on automatic detection techniques, datasets, and evaluation methods. Method:We conducted a systematic literature review (SLR) of 21 studies focused on detecting logging smells. In this review, we define key logging-related terms, identify and map the types of smells to an existing taxonomy, and examine the detection techniques, datasets, and evaluation strategies used across the studies. Results:We found that the research is still scattered and inconsistent. For example, there is no common benchmark or standardized approach for evaluating results, making it difficult to compare studies. In addition, we observe inconsistencies in the way log smells are addressed, as studies differ in the types and number of smells they target. Conclusion:There is still room for improvement in how logging smells are studied and detected. We point out several challenges and suggest future directions, such as developing better tools, using large language models (LLMs), and building more standardized datasets for evaluation.

</details>


### [116] [Engineering as Code: Bringing Software Engineering Methodology to Engineering Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04216)
*Song Difei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"工程即代码"（EaC）范式，将工程设计表达为文本原生的声明式语言ADL，并配以自动化规则引擎ESA实现设计时合规检查，在69条规则下检测耗时不到200ms，违规注入实验达到100%检出率和零误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成、芯片设计等可验证领域取得显著进展，但在建筑、机械工程和暖通空调等工程设计任务上仍然受限。作者认为根本原因不在于模型能力，而在于缺乏可被机器消费、验证和版本化的"设计即代码"可计算基础。现有CAD/BIM系统将设计逻辑与几何耦合导致质量保证后移，自动合规检查（ACC）存在几何误报和命名依赖问题。

Method: 提出Engineering as Code（EaC）范式，核心包括：(1)信息表示假说——工程AI瓶颈在于缺失可计算的设计表示而非模型能力；(2)ADL装配定义语言，以Part为原子，分为PDL（部件定义）、PML（部件配合）和PLL（部件布局）三个正交子语言；(3)ESA工程静态分析——将合规检查从下游审查移至设计阶段，基于语义类别（族类型、配合关系）而非几何进行操作，消除误报和命名依赖。

Result: 通过三个跨领域样本（电信机架扩展、模块化数据中心、机械键盘）验证了ADL表达力和ESA检测能力，检查69条规则耗时不到200ms。受控违规注入实验实现100%检出率和零误报，SD-HWE-Bench基准设计完成作为经验测试平台。

Conclusion: 工程设计的AI瓶颈可通过建立可计算的设计表示（Design as Code）来突破，EaC范式将合规检查前置到设计阶段，为工程设计领域的大模型应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Large language models have made significant progress in verifiable domains such as code generation and chip design, yet remain limited on engineering design tasks in fields like architecture, mechanical engineering, and HVAC. This paper argues the root cause is not model capability but the absence of a "Design as Code" computable foundation: design representations that can be consumed, verified, and versioned by machines. Existing CAD/BIM systems couple design logic with geometry, causing quality assurance to drift downstream; Automated Compliance Checking (ACC) suffers from geometric false positives and naming-dependent model reconstruction overhead. We propose the Engineering as Code (EaC) paradigm: expressing engineering design as text-native declarative language with automated rule engines, version control, and package management as quality gates. Core contributions: (1) the Information Representation Hypothesis, arguing the engineering AI bottleneck stems from missing computable design representations rather than model capability; (2) ADL (Assembly Definition Language) with Part as atom, organized into three orthogonal sub-languages -- PDL (Part Definition), PML (Part Mating), and PLL (Part Layout); (3) ESA (Engineering Static Analysis), shifting compliance checking from downstream review to design-time, operating on semantic categories (Family types, Mate relations) rather than geometry, eliminating false positives and naming dependencies. A prototype validates ADL expressiveness and ESA detection across three cross-domain samples (telecom rack expansion, modular datacenter, mechanical keyboard), checking 69 rules in under 200ms. A controlled violation injection experiment achieves 100% detection rate with zero false positives. The SD-HWE-Bench benchmark design is complete as an empirical testbed for the hypothesis.

</details>


### [117] [A Governance Methodology Layer for AI-Assisted Software Development: Defect Taxonomy, Controlled Ablation, and Process-Over-Capability Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04218)
*Sungjin Kwon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对自主编码智能体虽能高速通过语法检查（编译、类型安全、CI）但缺乏语义正确性保障的问题，提出四项贡献：缺陷分类学、运行时解耦的治理门控协议、方法论即代码的形式化验证框架，以及对比结构化审查与非结构化审查的受控消融实验。实验结果表明结构化审查在宽松和严格召回率上均优于非结构化审查（62% vs 50%，25% vs 0%），但两者都遗漏了文档质量缺陷，提示结构化的AI审查与人工流程审查具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码智能体可以高速生成通过语法检查的代码，但语法正确不等于语义正确——设计边界、安全不变量和维护性契约在自动化流水线中结构性不可见。现有工具缺乏对代码语义质量的治理机制，本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 本文提出四项核心方法：(1) 基于五种AI智能体权限与治理模块的缺陷分类学，区分静态分析可检测缺陷与需语义审查的缺陷；(2) 运行时解耦的治理门控——基于文件的协议，无API耦合地读取生成器输出并输出结构化裁决；(3) 方法论即代码——将验证协议表述为版本控制的、可执行的跨平台制品，采用两层架构分离可移植方法论与主机特定自动化；(4) 受控消融实验（E-ablation）比较基于结构化harness的审查与token匹配的非结构化审查提示。

Result: 在N=5个制品、8项独立真实值的消融实验中：结构化审查的宽松真实值召回率为62%（非结构化仅50%），严格召回率为25%（非结构化仅为0%）；结构化审查的严重性分级更一致，而非结构化审查会夸大严重性。两种条件均遗漏了人工QA审查员发现的文档质量缺陷，表明AI结构化审查与人工流程审查之间存在互补关系。

Conclusion: 在样本范围内，实验结果支持以下论点：在AI辅助软件开发中，审查流程的设计（而非审查模型的能力）是决定关键缺陷覆盖率的主导因素。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents produce output that passes syntactic checks -- compilation, type safety, CI -- at high velocity. Yet syntactic correctness does not imply semantic correctness: design boundaries, security invariants, and maintainability contracts remain structurally invisible to automated pipelines. This paper makes four contributions toward closing this gap. First, we present a defect-class taxonomy grounded in five AI agent permission and governance modules, distinguishing defects structurally detectable by static analysis from those requiring semantic review. Second, we describe a runtime-decoupled governance gate -- a file-based protocol that reads generator output and emits a structured verdict without API coupling, making it portable across code-generation tools. Third, we formalize methodology-as-code: expressing a verification protocol as a version-controlled, executable, cross-platform artifact with a two-layer architecture separating portable methodology from host-specific automation. Fourth, we report a controlled ablation experiment (E-ablation, N=5 artifacts, 8-item independent ground truth) comparing harness-structured review against a token-matched unstructured review prompt on the same artifacts. The structured condition records 62% lenient ground-truth recall against 50% for the unstructured condition, with 25% strict recall against 0%, and applies more consistent severity grades where the unstructured condition inflates severity. Both conditions miss document-quality defects identified by a human QA reviewer, indicating a complementarity between structured AI review and human process inspection. Within the bounds of this sample, the results are consistent with the thesis that review-process design, rather than reviewer-model capability, is the dominant factor in critical-defect coverage for AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [118] [Large Language Models for Fuzz Testing in Microservices: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04219)
*Ying Song,Ke Ping,Yuqing Wang,Xiaozhou Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对2024至2026年间发表的20项主要研究进行了系统性文献综述（SLR），探讨大语言模型（LLM）辅助的微服务模糊测试。研究发现LLM主要作为黑盒模糊测试中的语义输入生成器，并正向基于代理和检索增强的架构转变，能够改善有效输入生成并适度提升覆盖率和漏洞检测能力。然而评估实践仍不统一，存在基准标准化不足、成本报告稀缺以及偏向单服务实验等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统依赖异构API，其组合输入空间和状态依赖使传统模糊测试面临挑战，而LLM被引入以增强模糊测试的语义推理能力，但目前缺乏对该领域研究的系统性梳理与综合。

Method: 遵循既定的SLR指南，对2024至2026年间发表的20项主要研究进行系统分析，综合考察LLM在微服务模糊测试中的应用方式、评估方法与现存挑战。

Result: LLM主要用作黑盒模糊测试中的语义输入生成器，并出现向基于代理和检索增强架构转变的趋势；可改善有效输入生成，适度提高覆盖率与漏洞检测。但评估异质性明显，包括基准标准化不足、成本报告缺失以及偏向单服务实验。

Conclusion: 该综述提供了LLM角色与集成的分类体系、评估实践的整合视角，并将开放挑战映射到研究方向，为微服务中LLM驱动模糊测试的设计与部署提供支撑。

Abstract: Microservice systems (MSS) increasingly rely on heterogeneous APIs whose combinatorial input space and stateful dependencies challenge traditional fuzz testing. Meanwhile, Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently been introduced to enhance fuzzing with semantic reasoning over specifications, inputs, and runtime feedback. This paper presents a systematic literature review (SLR) of LLM-assisted fuzz testing for microservices to synthesise how LLMs are applied, evaluated, and what challenges remain. Following established SLR guidelines, we analyze 20 primary studies published between 2024 and 2026. Results show LLMs are mainly used as semantic input generators in black-box fuzzing, with a growing shift towards agent-based and retrieval-augmented architectures, improving valid input generation and modestly increasing coverage and vulnerability detection. However, evaluation remains heterogeneous, with limited benchmark standardization, scarce cost reporting, and a bias toward single-service experiments, highlighting a gap with real-world multi-service systems. This review provides a taxonomy of LLM roles and integrations, a consolidated view of evaluation practices, and a mapping of open challenges to research directions, supporting the design and deployment of LLM-driven fuzzing in microservices.

</details>


### [119] [Robustness and Trade-offs for Code LLMs on Protected Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04220)
*Jin Wen,Yuejun Guo,Yujie Ma,Qiang Hu,Maxime Cordy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对七个代码LLM在受保护（混淆）代码上的翻译与补全任务进行了基于执行的研究，覆盖多种编程语言、五种混淆方法和三种推理协议。结果表明，直接对混淆代码推理往往与对还原后代码推理效果相当甚至更优，高性能模型能保留约90%的Pass@1，显式还原通常不必要，模型能力是决定性因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码大语言模型越来越多地应用于可能被人为混淆的软件工件（如知识产权保护、逆向工程抵抗或访问控制场景）。反混淆通常被视为下游分析前的预处理步骤，但其对代码LLM流水线的实用性尚未在多个模型和保护方法间得到系统验证。

Method: 采用基于执行的实证研究方法，对七个代码LLM进行系统评估。实验涵盖C++、Go、Java和JavaScript四种源语言，五种混淆方法，以及三种推理协议（明文、混淆、反混淆后）。使用Pass@1作为核心度量，并在受控基准上比较不同模型能力和配置下的表现。

Result: 直接对混淆代码推理常与还原后推理持平或更优。高性能模型（如GPT-4.1和Qwen3-Coder-30B）在混淆翻译输入上保留约90%的Pass@1。同模型还原虽能恢复部分混淆导致的失败，但也会降低许多原本成功案例的性能，低能力模型净损失最大。模型能力是首要因素，源语言和混淆方法为次要但一致的影响因素。

Conclusion: 研究支持模型感知的流水线设计理念，并指出受保护代码工作流应主要使用基于执行的度量进行评估，而非仅依赖静态相似性指标。显式反混淆在多数高能力模型场景中并非必要步骤。

Abstract: Code large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used on software artifacts that may be intentionally obfuscated for intellectual-property protection, reverse-engineering resistance, or controlled access. In reverse engineering and security analysis, deobfuscation is commonly treated as the preprocessing step before downstream analysis or inference, yet its utility for code LLM pipelines has not been systematically validated across models and protection methods. We present an execution-based study of seven code LLMs on protected-code translation and completion, spanning source programs from C++, Go, Java, and JavaScript, five obfuscation methods, and three inference protocols: plain, obfuscated, and deobfuscated. Our results show that direct inference on obfuscated code often matches or exceeds inference on restored code. In this controlled benchmark, higher-capability models such as GPT-4.1 and Qwen3-Coder-30B retain about 90% Pass@1 on obfuscated translation inputs, indicating that explicit restoration is often unnecessary. Same-model restoration does recover some obfuscation-induced failures, but it also degrades many cases that already succeed, with lower-capability models showing the largest net losses. Across settings, model capability is the primary factor, while source language and obfuscation method have secondary but consistent effects. Overall, our findings support model-aware pipeline design and indicate that protected-code workflows should be evaluated primarily with execution-based metrics rather than static similarity alone.

</details>


### [120] [Automated Deployment of Real-Time Tasks for Phased Execution on Scratchpad-Based Multicore Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04221)
*Konstantin Dudzik,Maximilian Kirschner,Jürgen Becker*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型的部署方法论，自动化地将实时应用适配为分阶段执行（phased execution）模式，通过工具链扩展应用内存阶段并提供运行时环境，在RISC-V多核系统上以航空电子案例验证，仅需对应用实现进行最小修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核实时系统面临共享内存访问争用导致的干扰问题，分阶段执行虽能解决该问题，但内存阶段的管理和调度复杂度高，阻碍了其在实时应用中的采用。

Method: 提出模型驱动的自动化部署方法论，包含集成工具集成的部署流程，通过从生成的应用程序二进制文件中直接提取所需信息来扩展必要的内存阶段，并提供运行时环境来协调目标硬件平台上的执行。

Result: 在RISC-V多核系统上使用航空电子案例研究验证了该方法的可行性和关键组件及操作原理。

Conclusion: 该自动化部署方法能够降低分阶段执行在实时应用中采用的复杂度门槛，仅需对应用代码做最小改动即可实现。

Abstract: The increasing throughput demands in real-time systems and the consolidation of functionality on few, high-performance platforms are driving a shift towards parallel architectures. A key challenge for multicore real-time systems is the interference from contention for access to shared memory. Phased execution approaches address this challenge by restricting shared memory access to mutually exclusive memory phases, while computation is limited to memories local to each core. However, the complexity of managing and scheduling said memory phases hinders the adoption of phased execution in real-time applications. To address this challenge, we propose a model-based deployment methodology that automates the process of adapting applications to phased execution. We present an integrated tool-based deployment process that extends the application through the necessary memory phases and provides a runtime environment to orchestrate execution on the target hardware platform. Our approach requires minimal changes to the application implementation since the deployment tool directly extracts the required information from the generated application binaries. We validate our methodology using an avionics case study on a RISC-V multicore system, showcasing its key components and principles of operation.

</details>


### [121] [Physics-Direct FPGA Tooth-Contact Computation for Deterministic Gear Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04248)
*Jiacheng Miao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"physics-direct"方法，将齿轮齿面接触的闭式物理（接触点定位、主曲率提取、椭圆赫兹解）直接压缩为无分支的定点FPGA数据通路，通过开源openXC7流程实现并在XC7K480T芯片上验证。该方法无需训练数据，能在零训练条件下比神经网络更准确地外推载荷变化，延迟为编译期常数（零抖动），是嵌入式齿面接触估计中可解释、确定性的神经替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 齿轮数字孪生、硬件在环试验台和主动振动控制要求求解器具备实时性、确定性和可嵌入性，而传统LTCA（数据相关的线性互补问题LCP）在fp64下每个网格相位需耗时数秒，结构上无法满足实时要求；学习型代理模型虽然推理快，但每个训练样本都需要一次完整的LTCA求解，且无法保证训练范围外的泛化能力。

Method: 将闭式接触物理直接压缩为无分支的定点FPGA数据通路：包含四个SoC模块（几何、Hertz及两条融合通路），采用完全开源的openXC7流程（无需厂商工具、无需浮点IP），在退役的XC7K480T芯片上通过JTAG位精确回读验证。

Result: 片上前瞻跟踪LCP体压力误差在-22%以内，加载传动误差（LTE）峰峰值误差在-19%以内，精确保持W^{1/3}定律；零训练条件下载荷外推精度优于训练过的神经网络（18.9%对比20.3%）；延迟为编译期常数（σ=0抖动），基于DSP的几何核在约63个通道上饱和FPGA逻辑资源。

Conclusion: Physics-direct为嵌入式齿面接触估计提供了一种可解释、确定性强的替代方案，相比神经代理方法在泛化能力、确定性保障和硬件资源利用方面具有优势。

Abstract: Gear digital twins, hardware-in-the-loop rigs, and active vibration control close a loop around the instantaneous tooth-contact state, demanding a solver that is real-time, deterministic, and embeddable -- properties that loaded tooth contact analysis (LTCA), a data-dependent linear complementarity problem (LCP) costing seconds per mesh phase in fp64, structurally lacks; learned surrogates infer fast but spend one full LTCA solve per training sample and offer no guarantee beyond their training envelope. We instead compress the closed-form contact physics -- contact-point localization, principal-curvature extraction, and the elliptical Hertz solution -- directly into a branch-free fixed-point FPGA datapath, termed physics-direct, realized through the fully open-source openXC7 flow (no vendor tools, no floating-point IP) and validated on retired XC7K480T silicon. Four SoCs -- geometry, Hertz, and two fused paths -- pass bit-exact JTAG readback against a golden model. The on-chip preview tracks LCP body pressure to within -22% and loaded transmission error (LTE) peak-to-peak to -19%, preserving the exact $W^{1/3}$ law; with zero training it extrapolates in load more accurately than a trained network (18.9% versus 20.3%). Latency is a compile-time constant ($σ= 0$ jitter), and the DSP-bound geometry kernel saturates the fabric near 63 lanes. Physics-direct is an interpretable, deterministic alternative to neural surrogacy for embedded tooth-contact estimation.

</details>


### [122] [Reviewer Capability Governs Rejection Targeting, Not Repair Skill: Evidence from LLM Execute-Review-Revise Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04270)
*Faizan Tanveer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过控制验证者（评审者）的能力水平变化，在100道奥林匹克数学题上测试不同能力档次的评审模型对多智能体LLM流水线最终准确率的影响。研究发现：跨家族的中间档次评审者将准确率从52%提升到64%；同模型自我评审虽具有最高错误检测率（0.85召回率）但无显著增益，且更频繁地拒绝正确回答；低于能力下限时评审角色失效，仅使token成本翻倍而不改变任何答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM流水线中，不同能力等级模型被分配执行和验证等不同角色，因为所有阶段都使用旗舰模型成本过高。先前文献已验证阶段并非总是有益的，但评审者能力相对执行者基本固定。作者希望通过变化评审者能力水平，探究不同能力档次的验证模型对流水线结果的实际影响，从而为角色-能力配对提供实证依据。

Method: 在100道固定的奥林匹克数学问题上运行单一执行者-评审者配置。将评审者替换为从能力范围跨越到完全无法解题的多个模型（含跨家族中档模型和同模型自评），度量每次个体拒绝的最终结果，并与执行者能力进行交叉比较。

Result: 跨家族中档评审者使最终准确率从52%提升至64%（提高12个百分点，p=0.0005），且零损坏答案。同模型自我评审的错误检测率最高（召回率0.85），但拒绝次数是前者的2.1倍，修复率仅为三分之一（15%对43%），且错误拒绝自身35%的正确答案（跨家族评审者仅2%）。低损坏率实为"修订惰性"的假象：18个被错误拒绝的正确回答中，3个被执行者遵从的均变错，15个被忽略的则保持不变。最弱评审者改变零个最终答案，仅使token成本翻倍。

Conclusion: 评审者的能力选择至关重要：跨家族适度能力评审者优于同模型自评和过弱评审者；自我评审的高灵敏度反而因过度拒绝和低修复率而无益；存在能力下限，低于该下限评审角色完全失效。但本研究仅为单一配置、100道题的受控先导实验，不应泛化为关于验证阶段的一般性结论。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines increasingly assign roles, including execution and verification, to models of different capability tiers. This is done because running a flagship model at every stage is expensive. Previous literature has established that verification stages are not always beneficial, but holds reviewer capability roughly fixed relative to the executor. We vary it. We replace the reviewer with models spanning a capability range down to one that cannot solve the problems at all, and measure the outcome of every individual rejection. This is done across a constant set of 100 olympiad mathematics problems.
  A cross-family mid-tier reviewer improves final accuracy by 12 percentage points, from 52 to 64 percent (p = 0.0005), with zero damaged answers. Same-model self-review attains the highest error-detection rate of any condition (0.85 recall) yet yields no significant gain: it rejects 2.1 times as often for a third the repair rate (15 against 43 percent, p = 0.0074) and falsely rejects 35 percent of its own correct answers against 2 percent for the cross-family reviewer (paired p = 0.000015). The low damage rate of self-review proves to be an artifact of revision inertia rather than reviewer quality: of 18 falsely rejected correct answers, the three where the executor complied all became wrong, while the fifteen it ignored survived unchanged. Below a capability floor the role becomes inert: our weakest reviewer changed zero of 100 final answers while doubling token cost. These findings describe a single executor-reviewer configuration on 100 problems and should be read as a controlled pilot rather than a general claim about verification stages.

</details>


### [123] [Integrating Crash Report Mining and LLMs for Bug Localization and Repair: An Industrial Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04483)
*Marcos Medeiros,Uirá Kulesza,Christoph Treude,Daniel Lucena,Rafael Gomes,Roberta Coelho,Eiji Adachi,Rodrigo Bonifacio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了将崩溃报告挖掘技术（堆栈轨迹聚类和可疑文件/方法排序）与大规模语言模型（LLM）相结合，以支持工业软件系统中的崩溃定位与修复。在两个大型Java企业系统的38个崩溃缺陷上进行了回顾性评估，最佳配置能定位71%并正确修复52%的崩溃缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型工业软件系统中的崩溃报告缺陷分析需要大量维护工作，虽然已有研究表明LLM可辅助维护任务，但关于其在工业场景下辅助开发者分析崩溃报告缺陷和修复相关缺陷的有效性研究甚少。

Method: 作者将崩溃报告挖掘技术（堆栈轨迹聚类和可疑文件/方法排名）与LLM结合，先对5个LLM在4种提示配置下进行回顾性评估，选出最佳模型后在来自两个大型Java企业系统的38个崩溃缺陷上进行测试，并通过人工验证评估定位和修复效果。

Result: 最佳配置在完整数据集上能定位高达71%的崩溃缺陷，并正确修复52%的崩溃缺陷。

Conclusion: 将崩溃报告挖掘与基于LLM的修复相结合，能在工业维护工作流中有效支持调试活动，提供了实证证据。

Abstract: Analyzing crash-report bugs in large-scale industrial software systems requires substantial maintenance effort, particularly in production environments where developers must handle large volumes of crash reports and source code artifacts to localize and fix their root causes. While recent studies have shown that Large Language Models (LLMs) can assist with maintenance tasks, little is known about their effectiveness in supporting developers in analyzing crash-report bugs and repairing bugs associated with groups of crash reports in industrial settings. To address this gap, we investigate whether integrating crash report mining techniques---specifically stack trace clustering and suspicious file and method ranking---with LLMs can support crash localization and repair in production environments. We conduct a retrospective evaluation of five LLMs under four prompt configurations. After that, we chose the best model to run on 38 crash bugs collected from two large Java enterprise systems. We further analyze the structural characteristics and explanatory patterns of LLM-generated responses and assess localization and repair effectiveness through manual validation. Our results show that the best-performing configuration localizes up to 71% and correctly repairs 52% of crash bugs on the full dataset. These findings provide empirical evidence that combining crash report mining with LLM-based repair can effectively support debugging activities in industrial maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [124] [An Empirical Analysis of CodeQL False Positives and Query Refinements for Java Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04535)
*Amirali Sajadi,Saikat Dutta,Preetha Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析了CodeQL在Java安全分析中的误报模式，基于167个CVE实例构建了包含五个类别的源代码级误报分类法（路径约束遗漏、良性执行上下文、信任边界建模缺失等）。通过实施CodeQL优化，可消除81.8%的已审查误报。对于固定优化的泛化局限，评估了智能体编码工具，在提供模板指导下成功率可达56-62%，远高于无指导的28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAST工具（如CodeQL）在部署前帮助发现漏洞，但较高的误报率导致开发者需花费大量精力进行人工分类，这阻碍了工具的实际采用效率。

Method: 在110个项目的167个CVE实例上运行CodeQL Java安全查询套件，选取误报率最高的10个查询，人工审查500条误报路径并构建分类法；随后在查询级别实施CodeQL优化以过滤重复误报模式，并进一步评估两种智能体编码工具能否将优化模式自适应地迁移到新项目。

Result: 五位分类中"路径约束或消毒遗漏"占比最高（36.6%）。实施的CodeQL优化消除了81.8%的审查误报，在全量数据集中移除15.8%的路径和位置，同时保留8个真实阳性中的7个。智能体工具在提供模板指导时成功率为56%和62%，无指导时均为28%；有指导时编译通过率超过90%，无指导时降至30-36%。

Conclusion: 研究支持一种以优化为导向的SAST工作流程：将重复性误报建模到CodeQL查询中，并借助智能体工具自动适配到不同项目上下文，从而减少重复的人工分诊工作。固定优化方案虽有效但依赖项目特定上下文，智能体辅助适配是弥合这一泛化差距的可行路径。

Abstract: Static application security testing (SAST) tools help developers find vulnerabilities before deployment, but false positives create substantial triage effort. We study whether CodeQL false positives in Java security analysis form recurring, explainable patterns that can be reduced by refining the analysis. We run CodeQL's Java security query suite on 167 CVE instances from 110 projects, focusing on the ten queries with the highest false positive rates. We manually review 500 sampled false positive paths and locations and construct a source-level taxonomy. The five categories are Missed Path Constraint or Sanitization (36.6%), Benign Execution Context (29.4%), Missing Trust Boundary Modeling (27.6%), Imprecise Concurrency Modeling (5%), and Imprecise Sink Modeling (1.4%).
  Guided by these findings, we implement CodeQL refinements that detect and filter recurring false positive patterns at the query level. The refinements remove 81.8% of reviewed false positives. Across the full selected-query dataset, they remove 15.8% of reported paths and locations while retaining 7 of 8 true positives. This shows that many false positives can be reduced in the analysis, although fixed refinements often depend on project-specific context. To address this generalization gap, we evaluate whether agentic coding tools can adapt refinement patterns to new projects. Given our patterns as templates, the two tools succeed on 56% and 62% of tasks, with query compile-pass rates above 90%. Without this guidance, both succeed on only 28%, while compile rates fall to 30-36%. These results support a refinement-oriented SAST workflow in which recurring false positives are modeled in CodeQL queries and automatically adapted to different project contexts, reducing repeated triage.

</details>


### [125] [Dynamic Adaptation of the LLM Context for Generating Routines with Coupled Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04570)
*Gnaneswar Villuri,Hashmath Shaik,Alex Doboli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出动态上下文自适应（Dynamic Context Adaptation）方法，解决LLM代码生成中因"静态绑定"（执行依赖耦合）导致的失败问题。该方法通过验证智能体从执行轨迹提取结构化诊断信息，为生成智能体提供类似梯度的指导，并辅以知识图谱语义约束和模拟退火选择，在300/600/1000次评估预算下显著超越多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代码生成在面对执行依赖耦合问题时经常失败——一个例程的含义由另一个例程的运行时行为定义，这种关系无法仅从文本描述中解析。这种"静态绑定"限制广泛存在于跨组件优化、打包、路由和符号搜索等问题中。

Method: 提出动态上下文自适应框架，包含：1）验证智能体从执行轨迹提取结构化诊断信息，为生成提供类似梯度的引导；2）生成智能体每轮提出多个候选方案；3）从问题描述构建知识图谱提供语义约束；4）使用模拟退火选择候选以避免贪婪崩溃。

Result: 在8个问题中的7个上，该方法在300和600次评估时均优于zero-shot、Reflexion和OpenEvolve（p<0.01），处于群体搜索尚未积累足够多样性的预算区间。在核心动机问题（交叉耦合优化）上，1000次评估时也取得最佳分数。消融实验确认结构化执行反馈是主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 结构化执行反馈在正确性依赖于运行时耦合的场景下最具价值，验证了本文的核心假设。动态上下文自适应的验证-生成循环范式为这类静态绑定问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based code generation fails when correctness depends on execution-dependent coupling: the meaning of one routine is defined by the runtime behavior of another, a relationship that cannot be resolved from textual descriptions alone. This limitation, which we call static binding, is not confined to explicitly coupled problems; it appears to varying degrees whenever correctness depends on joint execution behavior across components, from explicit cross-coupled optimizers to subtler joint constraints in packing, routing, and symbolic search. This paper proposes dynamic context adaptation, a sample-efficient validation-generation loop designed for this setting. A validation agent extracts structured diagnostic information from execution traces, providing gradient-like guidance to a generation agent that proposes multiple candidates per iteration. A knowledge graph derived from the problem description supplies semantic constraints to the generation agent. Simulated annealing selects among candidates to avoid greedy collapse. Our method outperforms zero-shot, Reflexion, and OpenEvolve on seven of eight problems at both 300 and 600 evaluations (p < 0.01), a regime where population-based search has not yet accumulated sufficient diversity to compete. Notably, on the primary motivating problem (cross-coupled optimization), our method also achieves the best score at 1000 evaluations, consistent with the hypothesis that structured execution feedback is most beneficial when correctness depends on runtime coupling. Ablation results confirm that structured execution feedback is the primary driver.

</details>


### [126] [Software Engineering in the Agent Era From Trustworthy Change to Human Agent Software Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04630)
*Zhongjie Wang,Mingyi Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可测试的治理框架，用于管理由软件代理实现的"可扩展执行"。框架包含两个核心概念（可信变更TC和责任拓扑）与一个执行抽象（人-代理单元HAC），核心主张是：代理扩展了执行能力但未扩展权威，因此需要通过显式的责任架构来协调二者之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件代理使数字执行具有弹性——代码生成、测试、迁移等可大规模并行化，但问题框架、语义承诺、验证、整合等仍受限于人类认知和组织权威。由此出现的核心问题是：应如何治理这种规模化执行，使组织能够接受并维持其带来的变革？

Method: 提出一个双构念加一执行抽象的测试性框架：(1)可信变更（TC）——描述从意图到执行的工程对象；(2)责任拓扑——依据残余风险接受权限的分布对组织分类（单中心vs多锚点）；(3)人-代理单元（HAC）——产生候选方案、提案和证据，但执行不授予接受权限。

Result: 框架表明：执行与权威的扩展速度不同，分布式HAC会产生上下文一致性和失效压力；多锚点治理需要联合接受与显式责任收束。将权威作为明确分类轴后，可推导出变更状态、共享工程事实、验证和流程控制方面的推论。

Conclusion: 渐进式规范和有界容量分析仍是待检验的假设。本文贡献在于理论构建与操作化，经验有效性需通过受控、纵向和实地研究来验证。本文不声称发明了责任、问责或变更管理等概念，而是主张代理规模化的执行改变了这些概念之间的组合方式。

Abstract: Software agents make digital execution elastic: repository analysis, code generation, testing, migration, tool use, and operations can be replicated and parallelized without proportional human headcount. Problem framing, semantic commitment, verification, integration, attention, and residual-risk acceptance remain bounded by human cognition, organizational authority, and economic capacity. How should scalable execution be governed so organizations can accept and sustain its changes?
  Our testable framework has two constructs and one execution abstraction. Trustworthy Change (TC) is the engineering object moving from intent through delegated execution, verification, integration, acceptance, and operation. Responsibility Topology classifies organizations by the distribution of independent residual-risk acceptance authority. A single-center topology has one final baseline responsibility anchor; a multi-anchor topology requires joint acceptance across independently governed domains. The Human-Agent Cell (HAC) produces candidates, proposals, and evidence; execution grants no acceptance authority. As execution and authority scale differently, distributed HACs create context-coherence and invalidation pressures, while multi-anchor governance adds joint acceptance and explicit responsibility closure.
  Responsibility, accountability, change management, specification, verification, and human oversight predate this work; our claim is only that agent-scaled execution changes how they fit together. We make that authority an explicit classification axis and derive consequences for change state, shared engineering facts, verification, and flow control. Progressive Specification and bounded-capacity analysis remain hypotheses to test, not laws. We contribute theory construction and operationalization; empirical validity remains open to controlled, longitudinal, and field studies.

</details>


### [127] [How Developers Discuss Generative AI: A Longitudinal Study of the Visual Studio Code Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04680)
*Panida Rumriankit,Akito Monden,Hiroki Inayoshi,Pattara Leelaprute,Bundit Manaskasemsak,Kundjanasith Thonglek,Arnon Rungsawang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对VS Code GitHub仓库中2021年1月至2026年6月间的43,806个候选问题进行纵向分析，经关键词检索与语义过滤后得到25,227个AI相关问题，采用BERTopic识别讨论主题并分析其时间演变。结果显示开发者讨论聚焦于操作层面（代理管理、配置、可靠性、认证和计费），而调查研究中强调的风险（如幻觉和许可）很少出现；讨论从代码补全演变为对话式及代理式开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具（如GitHub Copilot、ChatGPT）已快速融入日常软件开发，但主流开源社区实际如何讨论它们仍缺乏实证研究。现有调查类研究对开发者感知的刻画可能与实际实践存在差异，需要从真实讨论数据出发补充视角。

Method: 收集43,806个候选issue；结合关键词检索与语义相关性过滤构建25,227个AI相关问题语料库；使用BERTopic识别讨论主题，并以过滤后语料进行主题验证和稳健性重聚类；通过月度流行率和Mann-Kendall趋势检验分析主题的时间演变。

Result: 开发者讨论以AI辅助开发环境运行的实践问题为主（代理管理、配置、可靠性、认证与计费等）；调查研究中常提及的风险（幻觉、许可）在issue中极少出现；讨论主题从AI辅助代码补全向对话式和代理式开发演变。

Conclusion: GitHub Issues为生成式AI提供了以实际工作流为导向的视角，与基于调查的开发者感知研究形成互补；VS Code issue跟踪器中的讨论主要聚焦于AI辅助软件开发的操作层面。

Abstract: Generative AI tools such as GitHub Copilot, ChatGPT, and coding agents have rapidly become part of everyday software development, yet little is known about how mainstream open source communities discuss them in practice. This paper presents a longitudinal analysis of generative-AI-related discussions in the Visual Studio Code (VS Code) GitHub repository, using 43,806 candidate issues created between January 2021 and June 2026. To improve corpus quality, we combined keyword retrieval with semantic relevance filtering, yielding a filtered corpus of 25,227 AI-related issues. We applied BERTopic to the retrieved corpus to identify discussion topics, using the filtered corpus for theme validation and a robustness re-clustering, and analyzed their evolution over time using monthly prevalence and Mann-Kendall trend tests. The results show that developer discussions are dominated by practical concerns regarding the operation of AI-assisted development environments, including agent management, configuration, reliability, authentication, and billing, whereas risks frequently emphasized in survey-based studies, such as hallucination and licensing, rarely surface in this venue. This suggests that discussions of generative AI in the VS Code issue tracker primarily focus on operational aspects of AI-assisted software development. Furthermore, discussions evolved from AI-assisted code completion toward conversational and agent-based development, reflecting the increasing integration of generative AI into software development workflows. These findings suggest that GitHub Issues provide a practical, workflow-oriented perspective on generative AI that complements survey-based studies of developer perceptions.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond Code Generation: Reliability, Verification, and Cost Economics in the Agentic Software Development Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04681)
*Happy Bhati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综合2024至2026年间的同行评审研究、基准审计与生产报告，概述AI编码系统从补全/聊天向自主智能体演进后，瓶颈从代码生成转移到测试、审查、部署等生产环节；提出四项工程概念（Agentic SDLC吞吐悖论、生产合格变更、验证税、Agentic SDLC控制面），将核心研究问题从"能生成多少代码"转向"每美元、每审查人时与每单位运行风险能交付多少生产合格价值"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理虽然显著提升了编程活动效率，但近期证据表明这些增益在从写代码到交付可靠软件之间大幅衰减。审查、集成、测试、部署等环节仍是瓶颈，同时成本结构从固定许可证转向可变的token/工具/沙箱/CI/返工成本，需要重新审视软件开发交付的核心瓶颈与经济学。

Method: 本文不提出新的模型实验，而是综合同行评审软件工程研究、大学研究、基准审计、大型科技公司生产报告、开发者遥测及成本管理证据（2024至2026年9月），提出四项概念性框架：Agentic SDLC吞吐悖论、生产合格变更（PQC）、验证税及Agentic SDLC控制面，并绘制从当前受监督代理到未来策略约束软件工厂的演进路线图。

Result: 研究表明AI在编码活动中的增益在通向可靠软件交付的过程中显著衰减；生产环节仍然是主要约束。经济模型正从可预测的人均许可转向可变的token、工具、沙盒、CI和返工成本。论文提出了一套概念框架和证据驱动的演进路线图来分析这一转变。

Conclusion: 核心研究问题应从"智能体能生成多少代码"转变为"每单位成本、审查人时和运行风险下，工程系统能交付多少生产合格价值"。未来的方向是由策略约束的软件工厂取代当前的受监督代理模式，需要建立Agentic SDLC控制面来自主性分配与成本、可靠性和人类注意力预算之间取得平衡。

Abstract: AI coding systems are moving from autocomplete and chat toward agents that can inspect repositories, edit multiple files, run tools, write tests, open pull requests, and work for long periods with limited supervision. This capability changes the bottleneck in software delivery. Recent field studies show meaningful gains in coding activity, but newer evidence also shows that those gains attenuate sharply between writing code and shipping reliable software. Review, integration, testing, security, deployment, and production operations remain constraining stages, while the economics are shifting from predictable per-seat licensing toward variable token, tool, sandbox, CI, and rework costs.
  This paper synthesizes peer-reviewed software-engineering research, university studies, benchmark audits, production reports from major technology companies, developer telemetry, and cost-management evidence released primarily from 2024 through September 2026. No new model experiment is claimed; numerical findings remain attributed to their original studies. The synthesis proposes four engineering concepts: the Agentic SDLC Throughput Paradox, Production-Qualified Change (PQC), the Verification Tax, and an Agentic SDLC Control Plane that allocates autonomy subject to cost, reliability, and human-attention budgets. An evidence-based horizon then maps today's supervised agents to future policy-bounded software factories. The central research question shifts from how much code an agent can generate to how much production-qualified value an engineering system can deliver per dollar, per reviewer-hour, and per unit of operational risk.

</details>


### [129] [Building a research-software catalog with a coding agent: from hackathon prototype to public deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04711)
*Kazuyoshi Yoshimi,Satoshi Terasaki,Gotai Yamada*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将生成式AI与编码代理用于研究软件门户开发的实践：在三天的黑客马拉松中构建了一个仓库目录原型，随后通过对抗性审查、数据质量检查等工程手段使其可公开发布，并将经验迁移至MateriApps大型人工策展门户的检索代理开发中。研究发现，AI辅助软件开发能显著加快速度，但最棘手的问题并非系统崩溃，而是产生看似合理实则不完整或错误输出的"静默失败"，因此需要显式验证、监控和反复审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI和编码代理虽然能加速研究软件的开发，但也增加了软件发现与维护的难度和需求，亟需探索如何高效构建和维护研究软件门户。

Method: 在三天的黑客马拉松中构建仓库目录原型，通过对抗性审查、数据质量检查、浏览器级验证和发布安全措施进行工程化改进；随后将经验应用于MateriApps门户，开发结合策展元数据、外部文档、向量搜索和本地语言模型生成的检索代理。

Result: 使用编码代理开发速度虽快，但实现可靠运行需要大量额外工程投入；主要问题不是崩溃而是产生看似合理但含静默错误（不完整或不正确）的输出，源于数据获取不完整、误导性评估以及检索或预处理失败。

Conclusion: AI辅助软件门户需要显式验证、监控和反复审查，策展元数据和维护良好的文档仍然不可或缺；虽然MateriApps相关工作仍处于探索阶段，但结合策展元数据、自动收集文档和检索辅助的方式对扩展其他研究软件门户具有潜在参考价值。

Abstract: Generative AI and coding agents can accelerate research software development, but they also increase the need for efficient software discovery and maintenance. We developed a repository catalog during a three-day hackathon and subsequently examined the engineering required to make it suitable for public deployment, including adversarial review, data-quality checks, browser-level validation, and publication safeguards. We then explored whether the lessons learned from this prototype could be transferred to a much larger, human-curated portal, through a retrieval agent under development for MateriApps that combines curated portal metadata, external documentation, vector search, and local language-model generation. Implementation with coding agents was rapid, but achieving reliable operation required substantial additional engineering: the most consequential problems were not crashes but silent failures that produced plausible yet incomplete or incorrect outputs, arising from incomplete data acquisition, misleading assessments, and retrieval or preprocessing failures. These observations suggest that AI-assisted software portals require explicit validation, monitoring, and repeated review, and that curated metadata and maintained documentation remain essential. The MateriApps work is exploratory and remains under active development, so the observations reported for it are preliminary; a comparable combination of curated metadata, automatically collected documentation, and retrieval-based assistance may nevertheless be useful for extending other research-software portals.

</details>


### [130] [Better Understanding, Better Fixes? A Study of Hallucination in LLM-based Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04909)
*Xuemeng Cai,Jiakun Liu,Linhan Yang,Wei Ma,Lingxiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对LLM自动程序修复(APR)过程中的幻觉现象进行了多层次分析，不仅关注最终补丁中的"修复幻觉"，还关注中间产物（触发测试用例识别、行覆盖率预测、额外测试用例生成）中的"理解幻觉"。基于832个Defects4J缺陷和三款代表性LLM的实验表明，幻觉在APR中普遍存在，只有21.0%-55.9%的补丁能通过开发者编写的测试套件，人工分析812个样本发现72.7%的修复案例存在修复幻觉，其中错误因果定位和错误修复策略分别占45.9%和18.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在程序修复中的评估主要关注最终结果，缺乏对修复过程中幻觉现象的系统性分析，而幻觉不仅出现在最终补丁中，也可能出现在指导补丁生成的中间产物中，需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 将APR中的幻觉分为两个层面：修复幻觉（最终补丁层面）和理解幻觉（中间产物层面），设计三项任务（触发测试用例识别、行覆盖率预测、额外测试用例生成），在832个Defects4J缺陷上对三款代表性LLM进行自动评估和人工分析。

Result: 修复和理解幻觉均普遍存在：仅21.0%-55.9%的补丁通过开发者测试套件；中间产物越准确与修复成功总体正相关但并非总是成立；人工分析发现72.7%的案例存在修复幻觉（错误因果定位45.9%、错误修复策略18.5%）；模型在触发测试识别、涉及分支控制流的行覆盖率预测及额外测试生成方面存在频繁错误。

Conclusion: LLM在APR中的幻觉是系统性的多层面问题，不仅体现在最终补丁上，也深入中间理解环节，需要同时关注修复过程各阶段的忠实性和可验证性。
